FILE .github/workflows/deploy-r2-media.yml
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+name: Deploy Breezze private R2 media
+
+on:
+  workflow_dispatch:
+
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+
+concurrency:
+  group: breezze-r2-media-deployment
+  cancel-in-progress: false
+
+jobs:
+  deploy:
+    name: Deploy rights-gated Cloudflare R2 Worker
+    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
+    environment: production
+    defaults:
+      run:
+        working-directory: infra/cloudflare/media-worker
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
+        with:
+          node-version: '22'
+      - name: Install and test media Worker
+        run: |
+          npm install --no-audit --no-fund
+          npm run check
+          npm test
+      - name: Validate account and HMAC configuration
+        env:
+          CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
+          CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
+          MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY: ${{ secrets.BREEZZE_MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY }}
+        run: |
+          test -n "$CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN"
+          test -n "$CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID"
+          test "${#MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY}" -ge 32
+      - name: Deploy Worker with private bucket and HMAC secret
+        id: worker
+        env:
+          CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
+          CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
+          MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY: ${{ secrets.BREEZZE_MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY }}
+          BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: ${{ vars.BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS }}
+        run: |
+          set -euo pipefail
+          printf '%s' "$MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY" | npx wrangler secret put MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY
+          deploy_args=()
+          if [ -n "${BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS:-}" ]; then
+            deploy_args+=(--var "WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS:$BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS")
+          fi
+          deploy_output="$(npx wrangler deploy "${deploy_args[@]}" 2>&1)"
+          printf '%s\n' "$deploy_output"
+          worker_url="$(printf '%s\n' "$deploy_output" | grep -Eo 'https://[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.workers\.dev' | tail -n 1 || true)"
+          test -n "$worker_url"
+          echo "worker_url=$worker_url" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
+      - name: Verify private worker never serves unsigned audio
+        env:
+          WORKER_URL: ${{ steps.worker.outputs.worker_url }}
+          BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: ${{ vars.BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS }}
+        run: |
+          set -euo pipefail
+          status="$(curl --silent --show-error --max-time 20 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "$WORKER_URL/audio/test-track/AAC_256")"
+          test "$status" = "403"
+          status="$(curl --silent --show-error --max-time 20 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "$WORKER_URL/")"
+          test "$status" = "404"
+          if [ -n "${BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS:-}" ]; then
+            origin="${BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS%%,*}"
+            curl --fail --silent --show-error -o /dev/null -D /tmp/r2-cors-headers -X OPTIONS \
+              "$WORKER_URL/audio/test-track/AAC_256" \
+              -H "Origin: $origin" \
+              -H "Access-Control-Request-Method: GET" \
+              -H "Access-Control-Request-Headers: range"
+            grep -Fiq "access-control-allow-origin: $origin" /tmp/r2-cors-headers
+          fi
+          echo "R2 Worker private/unsigned and CORS smoke checks passed."

+name: Listener runtime
+on:
+  pull_request:
+    paths:
+      - 'apps/mobile/**'
+      - 'packages/audio_contracts/**'
+      - 'packages/api_client/**'
+      - '.github/workflows/listener-runtime.yml'
+  workflow_dispatch:
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+env:
+  QA_PREVIEW_API_ORIGIN: https://breezze-api-edge.ykw8vmq7ph.workers.dev
+jobs:
+  android:
+    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
+    timeout-minutes: 45
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+      - uses: subosito/flutter-action@v2
+        with:
+          channel: stable
+          cache: true
+      - run: bash apps/mobile/tool/generate_platform_targets.sh
+      - name: Enable hosted Android acceleration
+        run: |
+          echo 'KERNEL=="kvm", GROUP="kvm", MODE="0666", OPTIONS+="static_node=kvm"' | sudo tee /etc/udev/rules.d/99-kvm4all.rules
+          sudo udevadm control --reload-rules
+          sudo udevadm trigger --name-match=kvm
+      - uses: reactivecircus/android-emulator-runner@v2
+        with:
+          api-level: 35
+          arch: x86_64
+          target: google_apis
+          profile: pixel_6
+          script: bash apps/mobile/tool/test_listener_runtime.sh emulator-5554
+  ios:
+    # Latest native video plugin needs Xcode 26; Intel runner avoids ARM64 queue stalls.
+    runs-on: macos-26-intel
+    timeout-minutes: 45
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+      - uses: subosito/flutter-action@v2
+        with:
+          channel: stable
+          cache: true
+      - run: bash apps/mobile/tool/generate_platform_targets.sh
+      - name: Boot iPhone and exercise listener flow
+        run: |
+          device=$(xcrun simctl list devices available --json | python3 -c 'import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin)["devices"]; print(next(p["udid"] for k in reversed(list(d)) if ".iOS-" in k for p in d[k] if p["name"].startswith("iPhone")))')
+          xcrun simctl boot "$device"
+          xcrun simctl bootstatus "$device" -b
+          bash apps/mobile/tool/test_listener_runtime.sh "$device"
+  licensed-media:
+    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
+    timeout-minutes: 15
+    services:
+      postgres:
+        image: postgres:16-alpine
+        env:
+          POSTGRES_USER: ci
+          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ci
+          POSTGRES_DB: listener_test
+        ports:
+          - 5432:5432
+        options: --health-cmd pg_isready --health-interval 5s --health-timeout 5s --health-retries 10
+    env:
+      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://ci:ci@127.0.0.1:5432/listener_test
+      MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY: isolated-local-worker-test-only-key
+      CDN_BASE_URL: http://127.0.0.1:8787
+      JWT_SECRET: isolated-local-listener-test-only-key
+      WRANGLER_SEND_METRICS: 'false'
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
+        with:
+          node-version: 22
+      - name: Start test API
+        working-directory: backend/api
+        run: |
+          npm install --no-audit --no-fund
+          npx prisma generate
+          npx prisma migrate deploy
+          npm run build
+          node dist/main > "$RUNNER_TEMP/api.log" 2>&1 &
+          curl -fsS --retry 15 --retry-all-errors --retry-delay 1 http://127.0.0.1:3000/health || { cat "$RUNNER_TEMP/api.log"; exit 1; }
+      - name: Load generated recording into local R2
+        working-directory: infra/cloudflare/media-worker
+        run: |
+          npm install --no-audit --no-fund
+          for variant in AAC_256 download; do
+            npx wrangler r2 object put "breezze-media/audio/24ead000-7bce-498c-806c-d6084a0ed108/$variant" --local --file="../../../apps/mobile/integration_test/fixtures/tone.m4a"
+          done
+          npx wrangler dev --local --ip 127.0.0.1 --port 8787 --var MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY:"$MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY" > "$RUNNER_TEMP/worker.log" 2>&1 &
+          for i in {1..30}; do
+            if curl -sS http://127.0.0.1:8787/ -o /dev/null; then exit 0; fi
+            sleep 1
+          done
+          cat "$RUNNER_TEMP/worker.log"
+          exit 1
+      - name: Verify stream and download grants through the media worker
+        working-directory: backend/api
+        run: node test/licensed-listener.mjs

 # Breezze
 
-Breezze is a free, listener-first music app built with one Flutter/Dart application codebase. It has five primary destinations: Home, Browse, Radio, Library and Search. Downloads live inside Library. There is no artist portal, artist upload workflow, subscription, membership, billing or paywall.
+Breezze is a free, listener-first music app built with one Flutter/Dart application codebase. It has four primary destinations: Home, Browse, Library and Search. Downloads live inside Library. There is no artist portal, artist upload workflow, subscription, membership, billing or paywall.
 
 ## Current client capabilities

@@ -0,0 +1,139 @@
+import 'dart:io';
+
+import 'package:breezze/core/audio/audio_service_bridge.dart';
+import 'package:breezze/core/audio/background_audio.dart';
+import 'package:breezze/features/player/player_controller.dart';
+import 'package:breezze/features/video/device_video.dart';
+import 'package:breezze/main.dart' as app;
+import 'package:breezze_api_client/breezze_api_client.dart';
+import 'package:breezze_audio_contracts/audio_contracts.dart' show PlaybackState;
+import 'package:file_selector/file_selector.dart';
+import 'package:material_ui/material_ui.dart';
+import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+import 'package:integration_test/integration_test.dart';
+import 'package:video_player/video_player.dart';
+
+class _FixtureLyrics implements LyricsHttp {
+  @override
+  Future<String> getText(String url) async => '[]';
+}
+
+void main() {
+  IntegrationTestWidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
+  testWidgets('download, offline decode, media controls and video handoff', (tester) async {
+    Future<void> until(String description, bool Function() ready, {int seconds = 25}) async {
+      final deadline = DateTime.now().add(Duration(seconds: seconds));
+      while (!ready()) {
+        if (DateTime.now().isAfter(deadline)) fail('Timed out waiting for $description');
+        await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 120));
+        await tester.pump();
+      }
+    }
+
+    app.main();
+    await until('app navigator', () => find.byType(Navigator).evaluate().isNotEmpty);
+    final navigator = tester.state<NavigatorState>(find.byType(Navigator).first);
+    final context = navigator.overlay!.context;
+    final container = ProviderScope.containerOf(context, listen: false);
+    final player = container.read(playerControllerProvider.notifier);
+    PlayerState current() => container.read(playerControllerProvider);
+    await until('native media session', () => container.read(backgroundBridgeProvider) is AudioServiceBridge);
+
+    final root = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('breezze-runtime-');
+    addTearDown(() async { await player.stop(); await root.delete(recursive: true); });
+    final library = DownloadsLibrary(root: root, lyrics: LyricsProvider(_FixtureLyrics()));
+    final tone = await rootBundle.load('integration_test/fixtures/tone.m4a');
+    final bytes = tone.buffer.asUint8List(tone.offsetInBytes, tone.lengthInBytes);
+    final server = await HttpServer.bind(InternetAddress.loopbackIPv4, 0);
+    server.listen((request) async {
+      request.response.contentLength = bytes.length;
+      request.response.add(bytes);
+      await request.response.close();
+    });
+    const track = BzTrack(id: 'fixture', title: 'Runtime tone', artist: 'Breezze QA', duration: Duration(seconds: 40));
+    try {
+      expect(await library.download(track, downloadUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:${server.port}/tone',
+        catalogTrackId: track.id, ownerId: 'listener', rightsExpiresAtUtc: DateTime.now().toUtc().add(const Duration(hours: 1))), isTrue);
+    } finally {
+      await server.close(force: true);
+    }
+    expect(await library.list(ownerId: 'another-listener'), isEmpty);
+    final saved = (await library.list(ownerId: 'listener')).single;
+    expect(saved.trackId, track.id);
+    expect(await File(saved.audioPath).readAsBytes(), orderedEquals(bytes));
+    await player.playTrack(trackId: saved.catalogTrackId, title: saved.title, localFilePath: saved.audioPath);
+    await until('offline audio position', () => current().position > const Duration(milliseconds: 600));
+    expect(current().playbackState, PlaybackState.playing);
+    expect(current().duration.inSeconds, closeTo(40, 1));
+
+    final bridge = container.read(backgroundBridgeProvider) as AudioServiceBridge;
+    await bridge.pause();
+    await until('paused', () => current().playbackState == PlaybackState.paused);
+    await bridge.seek(const Duration(seconds: 4));
+    await bridge.play();
+    await until('resumed after seek', () => current().position > const Duration(milliseconds: 4600));
+
+    final videoBytes = await rootBundle.load('integration_test/fixtures/clip.mp4');

FILE apps/mobile/lib/core/audio/authorized_download_grant.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,77 @@
+/// Validates backend-issued download grants before the client starts I/O.
+///
+/// Discovery metadata, preview URLs and missing/expired offline leases are
+/// never sufficient to authorize a persistent audio file.
+class AuthorizedDownloadGrant {
+  const AuthorizedDownloadGrant({
+    required this.downloadUrl,
+    required this.offlineLeaseExpiresAtUtc,
+    this.assetVersion,
+  });
+
+  final Uri downloadUrl;
+  final DateTime offlineLeaseExpiresAtUtc;
+
+  /// Stable, server-issued version of the *exact encoded media asset*.
+  /// Null means a partial download cannot be safely resumed.
+  final String? assetVersion;
+
+  static AuthorizedDownloadGrant? fromResponse(
+    Map<String, dynamic> response, {
+    DateTime? nowUtc,
+  }) {
+    if (response['allowed'] != true || response['previewOnly'] == true) {
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    final urlText = response['url'];
+    if (urlText is! String) return null;
+    final url = Uri.tryParse(urlText);
+    if (url == null || url.scheme != 'https' || url.host.isEmpty ||
+        url.userInfo.isNotEmpty || url.fragment.isNotEmpty) {
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    final now = (nowUtc ?? DateTime.now().toUtc()).toUtc();
+    DateTime? parseExpiry(Object? value) =>
+        value is String ? DateTime.tryParse(value)?.toUtc() : null;
+
+    final lease = parseExpiry(response['offlineLeaseExpiresAt']);
+    if (lease == null || !lease.isAfter(now)) return null;
+
+    // The API intentionally issues at most a 72h offline lease. A longer
+    // client-side lease is not proof of authority and must fail closed.
+    if (lease.isAfter(now.add(const Duration(hours: 72, seconds: 30)))) {
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    final signedUrlExpiryValue = response['expiresAt'];
+    if (signedUrlExpiryValue != null) {
+      final signedUrlExpiry = parseExpiry(signedUrlExpiryValue);
+      if (signedUrlExpiry == null || !signedUrlExpiry.isAfter(now)) return null;
+    }
+
+    final rightsExpiryValue = response['rightsExpiresAt'];
+    if (rightsExpiryValue != null) {
+      final rightsExpiry = parseExpiry(rightsExpiryValue);
+      if (rightsExpiry == null ||
+          !rightsExpiry.isAfter(now) ||
+          lease.isAfter(rightsExpiry)) {
+        return null;
+      }
+    }
+
+    final version = response['assetVersion'];
+    // Version identifiers are metadata, never raw URLs, tokens, or cookies.
+    final assetVersion = version is String &&
+            RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,128}$').hasMatch(version)
+        ? version
+        : null;
+
+    return AuthorizedDownloadGrant(
+      downloadUrl: url,
+      offlineLeaseExpiresAtUtc: lease,
+      assetVersion: assetVersion,
+    );
+  }
+}

+  /// A native playlist is safe only for local sources. Licensed streams

+        children: [
+          for (final source in sources) ja.AudioSource.file(source.streamUrl),
+        ],
+        useLazyPreparation: true, // prebuffer upcoming files, not the entire library
+      ),
+      initialIndex: initialIndex,
+    );
+  }

+    // just_audio switches from its native player to an idle player on stop.
+    // Finish that switch before disposing the platform during app shutdown.
+    try {
+      await _player.stop();
+    } finally {
+      await _player.dispose();
+    }
   }

FILE apps/mobile/lib/core/audio/offline_lease_policy.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,7 @@
+/// Local offline files are playable only while the backend-issued lease
+/// remains valid. A legacy metadata row without an expiry has no grant.
+bool hasValidOfflineLease(DateTime? expiresAtUtc, {DateTime? nowUtc}) {
+  if (expiresAtUtc == null) return false;
+  final now = (nowUtc ?? DateTime.now().toUtc()).toUtc();
+  return expiresAtUtc.toUtc().isAfter(now);
+}

FILE apps/mobile/lib/core/auth/native_social_sign_in.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,109 @@
+import 'dart:convert';
+import 'dart:math';
+
+import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
+import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
+import 'package:google_sign_in/google_sign_in.dart';
+import 'package:sign_in_with_apple/sign_in_with_apple.dart';
+
+/// Breezze never receives a Google/Apple password. The native platform prompt
+/// issues an identity token, which Supabase Auth verifies with that provider.
+/// Only a short-lived Supabase access token reaches the Breezze API bridge.
+class NativeSocialCredential {
+  const NativeSocialCredential(this.provider, this.idToken, {this.nonce});
+  final String provider;
+  final String idToken;
+  final String? nonce;
+}
+
+class NativeSocialSignInException implements Exception {
+  const NativeSocialSignInException(this.code);
+  final String code;
+}
+
+class NativeSocialSignIn {
+  static const googleWebClientId =
+      String.fromEnvironment('BREEZZE_GOOGLE_WEB_CLIENT_ID');
+  static const googleIosClientId =
+      String.fromEnvironment('BREEZZE_GOOGLE_IOS_CLIENT_ID');
+  static const appleEnabled =
+      bool.fromEnvironment('BREEZZE_APPLE_SIGN_IN_ENABLED');
+
+  Future<void>? _googleInitialization;
+
+  bool available(String provider) {
+    if (kIsWeb) return false;
+    if (provider == 'google') {
+      return googleWebClientId.isNotEmpty &&
+          (defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.android ||
+              defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.iOS);
+    }
+    if (provider == 'apple') {
+      return appleEnabled &&
+          (defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.iOS ||
+              defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.macOS);
+    }
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  Future<NativeSocialCredential> authenticate(String provider) async {
+    if (!available(provider)) {
+      throw const NativeSocialSignInException('SOCIAL_PROVIDER_NOT_CONFIGURED');
+    }
+    if (provider == 'google') {
+      try {
+        final signIn = GoogleSignIn.instance;
+        _googleInitialization ??= signIn.initialize(
+          clientId: defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.iOS &&
+                  googleIosClientId.isNotEmpty
+              ? googleIosClientId
+              : null,
+          serverClientId: googleWebClientId,
+        );
+        await _googleInitialization;
+        if (!signIn.supportsAuthenticate()) {
+          throw const NativeSocialSignInException('SOCIAL_PLATFORM_UNSUPPORTED');
+        }
+        final account = await signIn.authenticate();
+        final token = account.authentication.idToken;
+        if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
+          throw const NativeSocialSignInException('SOCIAL_TOKEN_INVALID');
+        }
+        return NativeSocialCredential('google', token);
+      } on NativeSocialSignInException {
+        rethrow;
+      } catch (_) {
+        throw const NativeSocialSignInException('SOCIAL_AUTH_FAILED');
+      }
+    }
+    if (provider == 'apple') {
+      final rawNonce = _randomNonce();
+      final hashedNonce = sha256.convert(utf8.encode(rawNonce)).toString();
+      try {
+        final credential = await SignInWithApple.getAppleIDCredential(
+          scopes: [
+            AppleIDAuthorizationScopes.email,
+            AppleIDAuthorizationScopes.fullName,
+          ],
+          nonce: hashedNonce,
+        );
+        final token = credential.identityToken;
+        if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
+          throw const NativeSocialSignInException('SOCIAL_TOKEN_INVALID');
+        }
+        return NativeSocialCredential('apple', token, nonce: rawNonce);
+      } on NativeSocialSignInException {
+        rethrow;
+      } catch (_) {
+        throw const NativeSocialSignInException('SOCIAL_AUTH_FAILED');
+      }
+    }
+    throw const NativeSocialSignInException('INVALID_PROVIDER');
+  }
+
+  String _randomNonce() {
+    const alphabet = '0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
+    final random = Random.secure();
+    return List.generate(32, (_) => alphabet[random.nextInt(alphabet.length)]).join();
+  }
+}

FILE apps/mobile/lib/core/auth/pending_logout_queue.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
+
+/// Pending server-side session revocations must survive a network outage
+/// without keeping the token usable as Breezze's active local session.
+abstract class PendingLogoutPersistence {
+  Future<Map<String, String>> readAll();
+  Future<void> put(String key, String value);
+  Future<void> delete(String key);
+}
+
+class SecurePendingLogoutPersistence implements PendingLogoutPersistence {
+  SecurePendingLogoutPersistence(this.storage);
+
+  final FlutterSecureStorage storage;
+
+  @override
+  Future<Map<String, String>> readAll() => storage.readAll();
+
+  @override
+  Future<void> put(String key, String value) =>
+      storage.write(key: key, value: value);
+
+  @override
+  Future<void> delete(String key) => storage.delete(key: key);
+}
+
+/// Stores refresh tokens *only* for future revocation in encrypted storage.
+/// These tokens are never restored as active credentials, exposed to the UI,
+/// exported, logged, or used to acquire new access tokens.
+class PendingLogoutQueue {
+  PendingLogoutQueue(this._storage);
+
+  static const prefix = 'breezze.pending-logout.';
+  final PendingLogoutPersistence _storage;
+  int _sequence = 0;
+
+  Future<void> enqueue(String refreshToken) async {
+    if (refreshToken.isEmpty) return;
+    final key = '$prefix${DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch}-${_sequence++}';
+    await _storage.put(key, refreshToken);
+  }
+
+  Future<int> flush(Future<void> Function(String token) revoke) async {
+    final all = await _storage.readAll();
+    var revokedCount = 0;
+    for (final entry in all.entries) {
+      if (!entry.key.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
+      try {
+        if (entry.value.isNotEmpty) await revoke(entry.value);
+        await _storage.delete(entry.key);
+        revokedCount++;
+      } catch (_) {
+        // Preserve this token for a future retry when the network recovers.
+      }
+    }
+    return revokedCount;
+  }
+}

FILE apps/mobile/lib/core/auth/supabase_auth_transport.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,129 @@
+import 'dart:convert';
+
+import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
+
+/// Sign-in transport for verified Supabase users. Only a PUBLIC publishable
+/// key belongs in the client. Returned Supabase access tokens are used once
+/// for backend identity exchange and are never persisted by this transport.
+class SupabaseAuthTransport {
+  SupabaseAuthTransport({
+    required this.projectUrl,
+    required this.publishableKey,
+    http.Client? client,
+  }) : _client = client ?? http.Client();
+
+  final String projectUrl;
+  final String publishableKey;
+  final http.Client _client;
+
+  Uri _endpoint(String path, [Map<String, String>? query]) {
+    final url = Uri.tryParse(projectUrl);
+    if (url == null ||
+        url.scheme != 'https' ||
+        !RegExp(r'^[a-z0-9-]+\.supabase\.co$').hasMatch(url.host) ||
+        url.path.isNotEmpty ||
+        url.hasQuery ||
+        url.hasFragment ||
+        publishableKey.isEmpty) {
+      throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('SUPABASE_AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED');
+    }
+    return url.replace(path: '/auth/v1$path', queryParameters: query);
+  }
+
+  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _send(Uri url, Map<String, dynamic> data) async {
+    final response = await _client
+        .post(url,
+          headers: {
+            'apikey': publishableKey,
+            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+          },
+          body: jsonEncode(data),
+        )
+        .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 12));
+    final Object? decoded;
+    try {
+      decoded = jsonDecode(response.body);
+    } catch (_) {
+      throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('SUPABASE_AUTH_INVALID_RESPONSE');
+    }
+    if (decoded is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
+      throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('SUPABASE_AUTH_INVALID_RESPONSE');
+    }
+    if (response.statusCode >= 400) {
+      // Never expose provider diagnostics containing PII or raw credentials
+      // as user-facing error messages.
+      if (response.statusCode == 400 || response.statusCode == 401) {
+        throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('INVALID_CREDENTIALS');
+      }
+      if (response.statusCode == 429) {
+        throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('RATE_LIMITED');
+      }
+      throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('SUPABASE_AUTH_UNAVAILABLE');
+    }
+    return decoded;
+  }
+
+  Future<String> signIn(String email, String password) async {
+    final data = await _send(
+      _endpoint('/token', {'grant_type': 'password'}),
+      {'email': email, 'password': password},
+    );
+    final token = data['access_token'];
+    if (token is! String || token.isEmpty) {
+      throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('SUPABASE_AUTH_INVALID_RESPONSE');
+    }
+    return token;
+  }
+
+  /// Native provider identity token, exchanged only with the configured
+  /// Supabase Auth origin. No Google/Apple token is persisted on device.
+  Future<String> signInWithIdToken({
+    required String provider,
+    required String idToken,
+    String? nonce,
+  }) async {
+    if (provider != 'google' && provider != 'apple') {
+      throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('INVALID_PROVIDER');
+    }
+    if (idToken.isEmpty || idToken.length > 16000) {
+      throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('SOCIAL_TOKEN_INVALID');
+    }
+    final result = await _send(
+      _endpoint('/token', {'grant_type': 'id_token'}),
+      {
+        'provider': provider,
+        'id_token': idToken,
+        if (nonce != null) 'nonce': nonce,
+      },
+    );
+    final token = result['access_token'];
+    if (token is! String || token.isEmpty) {
+      throw const SupabaseAuthTransportException('SUPABASE_AUTH_INVALID_RESPONSE');
+    }
+    return token;
+  }
+
+  /// The reset message has a link to the approved Breezze recovery page.
+  /// The response deliberately does not reveal whether an email exists.
+  Future<void> requestPasswordReset(String email) async {
+    await _send(_endpoint('/recover'), {'email': email.trim()});
+  }
+
+  /// With email confirmation enabled Supabase returns a user but no session.
+  /// The UI must ask the listener to confirm the email, never fake success.
+  Future<String?> signUp(String email, String password, {String? displayName}) async {
+    final data = await _send(_endpoint('/signup'), {
+      'email': email,
+      'password': password,
+      if (displayName != null && displayName.trim().isNotEmpty)
+        'data': {'display_name': displayName.trim()},
+    });
+    final token = data['access_token'];
+    return token is String && token.isNotEmpty ? token : null;
+  }
+}
+
+class SupabaseAuthTransportException implements Exception {
+  const SupabaseAuthTransportException(this.code);
+  final String code;
+}

+  void _disarmNativeGapless() {
+    _gaplessEngine = null;
+    _gaplessEntries = null;
+    _gaplessIndex = null;
+  }
+
+  void _syncNativeGaplessIndex(int index) {
+    final entries = _gaplessEntries;
+    if (entries == null || index < 0 || index >= entries.length ||
+        _gaplessIndex == index) {
+      return;
+    }
+    _gaplessIndex = index;
+    final entry = entries[index];
+    final previousId = state.currentTrack?.id;
+    if (previousId != null) _scrobbles.onStop(previousId);
+    if (!_queue.selectEntry(entry.entryId)) return;
+    final next = _trackInfoFor(entry.trackId) ?? TrackInfo(id: entry.trackId);
+    state = state.copyWith(
+      currentTrack: next,
+      position: Duration.zero,
+      duration: next.duration,
+    );
+    if (state.playbackState == PlaybackState.playing) {
+      _scrobbles.onPlay(entry.trackId, DateTime.now().toUtc());
+    }
+  }
+
+  /// Editing a playing native playlist needs a fresh single-item source.
+  /// Never let a stale decoder queue disagree with the global queue.
+  void _invalidateNativeGapless() {
+    if (_gaplessEngine == null) return;
+    final track = state.currentTrack;
+    final position = state.position;
+    _disarmNativeGapless();
+    if (track != null) {
+      unawaited(_resolveAndPlay(track, initialPosition: position));
+    }
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _resolveAndPlayLocalPlaylist(
+    List<TrackInfo> tracks,
+    List<QueueEntry> entries,
+    int index,
+    GaplessLocalQueueEngine native,
+  ) async {
+    final requestId = ++_requestGeneration;
+    _disarmNativeGapless();
+    final selected = tracks[index];
+    final oldId = state.currentTrack?.id;
+    if (oldId != null) _scrobbles.onStop(oldId);
+    unawaited(_closePlaybackSession());
+    _autoAdvancedTrackId = null;
+    final source = PlayableSource(
+      trackId: selected.id,
+      streamUrl: selected.localFilePath!,
+      expiresAtUtc: DateTime.utc(9999),
+      isLocal: true,
+    );
+    _activeSource = source;
+    state = state.copyWith(
+      currentTrack: selected,
+      position: Duration.zero,
+      duration: selected.duration,
+    );
+    _machine.dispatch(StartRequested(selected.id, requestId: requestId));
+    _machine.dispatch(SourceResolved(
+      selected.id, source.streamUrl, requestId: requestId,
+    ));
+    if (_machine.state != PlaybackState.loading) return;
+    _gaplessEngine = native;
+    _gaplessEntries = List.of(entries);
+    _gaplessIndex = index;
+
+    try {
+      await _serializeEngineOperation(() async {
+        if (!_isCurrentRequest(requestId) || !identical(_engine, native)) return;
+        await native.prepareLocalPlaylist([
+          for (final track in tracks)
+            PlayableSource(
+              trackId: track.id,
+              streamUrl: track.localFilePath!,
+              expiresAtUtc: DateTime.utc(9999),
+              isLocal: true,
+            ),
+        ], initialIndex: index);
+        if (!_isCurrentRequest(requestId) || !identical(_engine, native)) return;
+        _machine.dispatch(MediaReady(requestId: requestId));
+        if (_machine.state != PlaybackState.buffering) return;
+        _machine.dispatch(PlayPressed());
+        if (_machine.state != PlaybackState.playing) return;
+        unawaited(native.play().catchError((Object error, StackTrace stack) {
+          if (!_isCurrentRequest(requestId)) return;
+          _disarmNativeGapless();
+          _machine.dispatch(SourceResolutionFailed(
+            'LOCAL_GAPLESS_PLAYBACK_FAILED', requestId: requestId,
+          ));
+        }));

FILE apps/mobile/lib/features/settings/account_sign_out.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+import 'package:material_ui/material_ui.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
+
+import '../../core/auth/auth_session.dart';
+
+enum _SignOutScope { thisDevice, allDevices }
+
+/// Shared sign-out confirmation for Settings and the account page.
+/// The all-devices action requires a server acknowledgement before local
+/// credentials are erased; an offline client cannot revoke remote devices.
+Future<void> confirmBreezzeSignOut(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
+  final scope = await showDialog<_SignOutScope>(
+    context: context,
+    builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
+      title: const Text('Sign out of Breezze?'),
+      content: const Text(
+        'Your saved account data will stay private. Sign out of all devices '
+        'requires an internet connection and will end every active session.',
+      ),
+      actions: [
+        TextButton(
+          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(),
+          child: const Text('Cancel'),
+        ),
+        TextButton(
+          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(_SignOutScope.thisDevice),
+          child: const Text('This device'),
+        ),
+        FilledButton(
+          onPressed: () => Navigator.of(dialogContext).pop(_SignOutScope.allDevices),
+          child: const Text('All devices'),
+        ),
+      ],
+    ),
+  );
+  if (!context.mounted || scope == null) return;
+  try {
+    await ref.read(authSessionProvider.notifier).signOut(
+      allDevices: scope == _SignOutScope.allDevices,
+    );
+  } catch (_) {
+    if (!context.mounted) return;
+    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
+      const SnackBar(content: Text(
+        'Could not revoke all sessions. Check your connection and retry.',
+      )),
+    );
+  }
+}

+/// Audio may be restored only if the video still belongs to the exact
+/// queue entry and recording that were playing at handoff time.
+/// Never resurrect a superseded track or override a user/OS pause.
+bool shouldResumeAudioAfterVideo({
+  required bool wasPlaying,
+  required String? openedEntryId,
+  required String? openedTrackId,
+  required String? currentEntryId,
+  required String? currentTrackId,
+  required PlaybackState currentState,
+  required AppLifecycleState? lifecycleState,
+}) =>
+    wasPlaying &&
+    openedEntryId != null &&
+    openedEntryId == currentEntryId &&
+    openedTrackId != null &&
+    openedTrackId == currentTrackId &&
+    currentState == PlaybackState.paused &&
+    lifecycleState == AppLifecycleState.resumed;
+

FILE apps/mobile/test/core/api/api_timeout_test.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,31 @@
+import 'dart:async';
+
+import 'package:breezze/core/api/breezze_api.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
+import 'package:http/testing.dart';
+
+void main() {
+  test('all HTTP methods have a bounded timeout for offline recovery', () async {
+    final client = MockClient((_) async {
+      await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
+      return http.Response('{}', 200);
+    });
+    final api = BreezzeApi(
+      baseUrl: 'https://api.example.test',
+      client: client,
+      requestTimeout: const Duration(milliseconds: 15),
+    );
+    await expectLater(api.homeFeed(), throwsA(isA<TimeoutException>()));
+    await expectLater(api.logout('expired-session-token'), throwsA(isA<TimeoutException>()));
+    await expectLater(
+      api.updateProfile(displayName: 'Listener'),
+      throwsA(isA<TimeoutException>()),
+    );
+    await expectLater(
+      api.deleteAccount(password: 'redacted'),
+      throwsA(isA<TimeoutException>()),
+    );
+    client.close();
+  });
+}

FILE apps/mobile/test/core/audio/authorized_download_grant_test.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,71 @@
+import 'package:breezze/core/audio/authorized_download_grant.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+
+void main() {
+  final now = DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 23, 10);
+  final signedExpiry = now.add(const Duration(minutes: 5));
+  final leaseExpiry = now.add(const Duration(hours: 6));
+  final rightsExpiry = now.add(const Duration(hours: 8));
+
+  Map<String, dynamic> grant() => {
+    'allowed': true,
+    'url': 'https://media.example.org/download?sig=redacted',
+    'expiresAt': signedExpiry.toIso8601String(),
+    'offlineLeaseExpiresAt': leaseExpiry.toIso8601String(),
+    'rightsExpiresAt': rightsExpiry.toIso8601String(),
+    'assetVersion': 'master-42:aac256',
+  };
+
+  test('accepts short lived URL with bounded offline lease', () {
+    final result = AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(
+      grant(), nowUtc: now,
+    );
+    expect(result, isNotNull);
+    expect(result!.offlineLeaseExpiresAtUtc, leaseExpiry);
+    expect(result.assetVersion, 'master-42:aac256');
+  });
+
+  test('rejects missing, expired or overly long offline lease', () {
+    final missing = grant()..remove('offlineLeaseExpiresAt');
+    expect(AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(missing, nowUtc: now), isNull);
+    final expired = grant()..['offlineLeaseExpiresAt'] =
+        now.subtract(const Duration(seconds: 1)).toIso8601String();
+    expect(AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(expired, nowUtc: now), isNull);
+    final excessive = grant()..['offlineLeaseExpiresAt'] =
+        now.add(const Duration(days: 4)).toIso8601String();
+    expect(AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(excessive, nowUtc: now), isNull);
+  });
+
+  test('rejects expired signed URL or lease beyond rights end', () {
+    final stale = grant()..['expiresAt'] =
+        now.subtract(const Duration(seconds: 1)).toIso8601String();
+    expect(AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(stale, nowUtc: now), isNull);
+    final overRights = grant()..['rightsExpiresAt'] =
+        now.add(const Duration(minutes: 20)).toIso8601String();
+    expect(AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(overRights, nowUtc: now), isNull);
+  });
+
+  test('rejects untrusted or preview-only sources', () {
+    final preview = grant()..['previewOnly'] = true;
+    expect(AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(preview, nowUtc: now), isNull);
+    final denied = grant()..['allowed'] = false;
+    expect(AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(denied, nowUtc: now), isNull);
+    for (final url in [
+      'http://media.example.org/download',
+      'https://user:pass@media.example.org/download',
+      'https://media.example.org/download#fragment',
+      'file:///tmp/download',
+    ]) {
+      expect(AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(
+        grant()..['url'] = url, nowUtc: now,
+      ), isNull, reason: url);
+    }
+  });
+
+  test('untrusted asset version does not enable partial resume', () {
+    final data = grant()..['assetVersion'] = 'https://other.invalid/media?token=secret';
+    final result = AuthorizedDownloadGrant.fromResponse(data, nowUtc: now);
+    expect(result, isNotNull);
+    expect(result!.assetVersion, isNull);
+  });
+}

FILE apps/mobile/test/core/audio/offline_lease_policy_test.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+import 'package:breezze/core/audio/offline_lease_policy.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+
+void main() {
+  final now = DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 23, 12);
+
+  test('missing offline lease is never a perpetual entitlement', () {
+    expect(hasValidOfflineLease(null, nowUtc: now), isFalse);
+  });
+
+  test('expired or exact-boundary lease cannot play', () {
+    expect(
+      hasValidOfflineLease(now.subtract(const Duration(milliseconds: 1)),
+          nowUtc: now), isFalse,
+    );
+    expect(hasValidOfflineLease(now, nowUtc: now), isFalse);
+  });
+
+  test('future server-issued lease permits local playback', () {
+    expect(
+      hasValidOfflineLease(now.add(const Duration(hours: 4)), nowUtc: now),
+      isTrue,
+    );
+  });
+
+  test('offset-aware lease comparison always uses UTC', () {
+    final withOffset = DateTime.parse('2026-11-23T15:00:01+03:00');
+    expect(hasValidOfflineLease(withOffset, nowUtc: now), isTrue);
+  });
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import 'package:breezze/core/auth/pending_logout_queue.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+
+class _MemoryPendingLogoutPersistence implements PendingLogoutPersistence {
+  final values = <String, String>{};
+
+  @override
+  Future<Map<String, String>> readAll() async => Map.of(values);
+
+  @override
+  Future<void> put(String key, String value) async {
+    values[key] = value;
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Future<void> delete(String key) async {
+    values.remove(key);
+  }
+}
+
+void main() {
+  test('offline revocations survive failure and are replayed independently', () async {
+    final storage = _MemoryPendingLogoutPersistence();
+    storage.values['breezze.refresh_token'] = 'active-session-not-for-replay';
+    final queue = PendingLogoutQueue(storage);
+    await queue.enqueue('offline-user-a');
+    await queue.enqueue('offline-user-b');
+
+    final delivered = <String>[];
+    final successes = await queue.flush((token) async {
+      if (token == 'offline-user-a') throw StateError('network disconnected');
+      delivered.add(token);
+    });
+    expect(successes, 1);
+    expect(delivered, ['offline-user-b']);
+    expect(storage.values.values, contains('offline-user-a'));
+    expect(storage.values.values, isNot(contains('offline-user-b')));
+    expect(storage.values['breezze.refresh_token'], 'active-session-not-for-replay');
+
+    expect(await queue.flush((token) async => delivered.add(token)), 1);
+    expect(delivered, ['offline-user-b', 'offline-user-a']);
+    expect(storage.values.keys.where((k) => k.startsWith(PendingLogoutQueue.prefix)), isEmpty);
+  });
+}

FILE apps/mobile/test/core/auth/supabase_auth_transport_test.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,132 @@
+import 'dart:convert';
+
+import 'package:breezze/core/auth/supabase_auth_transport.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
+import 'package:http/testing.dart';
+
+void main() {
+  test('Supabase password sign-in uses only a public key and returns an ephemeral JWT', () async {
+    late http.Request sent;
+    final transport = SupabaseAuthTransport(
+      projectUrl: 'https://breezzetest.supabase.co',
+      publishableKey: 'sb_publishable_test',
+      client: MockClient((request) async {
+        sent = request;
+        return http.Response(jsonEncode({'access_token': 'temporary-user-jwt'}), 200);
+      }),
+    );
+
+    expect(await transport.signIn('listener@example.test', 'password'), 'temporary-user-jwt');
+    expect(sent.url.toString(),
+        'https://breezzetest.supabase.co/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password');
+    expect(sent.headers['apikey'], 'sb_publishable_test');
+    expect(jsonDecode(sent.body)['email'], 'listener@example.test');
+    expect(sent.headers.containsKey('Authorization'), false);
+  });
+
+  test('unverified signup requests email confirmation instead of claiming an active login', () async {
+    final transport = SupabaseAuthTransport(
+      projectUrl: 'https://breezzetest.supabase.co',
+      publishableKey: 'sb_publishable_test',
+      client: MockClient((request) async {
+        expect(request.url.path, '/auth/v1/signup');
+        expect(jsonDecode(request.body)['data']['display_name'], 'Breezze Listener');
+        return http.Response('{"user":{"id":"some-user"},"session":null}', 200);
+      }),
+    );
+    expect(await transport.signUp(
+      'listener@example.test',
+      'a-secure-password',
+      displayName: 'Breezze Listener',
+    ), isNull);
+  });
+
+  test('wrong credentials return a generic error without leaking provider body', () async {
+    final transport = SupabaseAuthTransport(
+      projectUrl: 'https://breezzetest.supabase.co',
+      publishableKey: 'sb_publishable_test',
+      client: MockClient((_) async => http.Response(
+        '{"msg":"contains private information"}', 400,
+      )),
+    );
+    await expectLater(transport.signIn('a@example.test', 'bad'), throwsA(
+      isA<SupabaseAuthTransportException>().having(
+        (value) => value.code, 'code', 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS',
+      ),
+    ));
+  });
+
+  test('rejects a Supabase lookalike host before sending credentials', () async {
+    var requested = false;
+    final transport = SupabaseAuthTransport(
+      projectUrl: 'https://breezzetest.supabase.co.evil.example',
+      publishableKey: 'sb_publishable_test',
+      client: MockClient((_) async {
+        requested = true;
+        return http.Response('{}', 200);
+      }),
+    );
+    await expectLater(transport.signIn('a@example.test', 'password'), throwsA(
+      isA<SupabaseAuthTransportException>(),
+    ));
+    expect(requested, isFalse);
+  });
+  test('Google ID-token grant validates through Supabase Auth, never a second provider backend', () async {
+    late http.Request sent;
+    final transport = SupabaseAuthTransport(
+      projectUrl: 'https://breezzetest.supabase.co',
+      publishableKey: 'sb_publishable_test',
+      client: MockClient((request) async {
+        sent = request;
+        return http.Response('{"access_token":"ephemeral-supabase-jwt"}', 200);
+      }),
+    );
+    expect(await transport.signInWithIdToken(
+      provider: 'google', idToken: 'signed-google-id-token',
+    ), 'ephemeral-supabase-jwt');
+    expect(sent.url.path, '/auth/v1/token');
+    expect(sent.url.queryParameters['grant_type'], 'id_token');
+    expect(sent.headers['apikey'], 'sb_publishable_test');
+    expect(jsonDecode(sent.body), {
+      'provider': 'google', 'id_token': 'signed-google-id-token',
+    });
+  });
+
+  test('Apple passes raw nonce to Supabase while native Apple receives SHA256', () async {
+    late http.Request sent;
+    final transport = SupabaseAuthTransport(
+      projectUrl: 'https://breezzetest.supabase.co',
+      publishableKey: 'sb_publishable_test',
+      client: MockClient((request) async {
+        sent = request;
+        return http.Response('{"access_token":"verified-jwt"}', 200);
+      }),
+    );
+    await transport.signInWithIdToken(
+      provider: 'apple', idToken: 'signed-apple-id-token',
+      nonce: 'secure-raw-nonce',
+    );
+    expect(jsonDecode(sent.body), {
+      'provider': 'apple', 'id_token': 'signed-apple-id-token',
+      'nonce': 'secure-raw-nonce',
+    });
+  });
+
+  test('other identity providers are denied before sending any request', () async {
+    var requests = 0;
+    final transport = SupabaseAuthTransport(
+      projectUrl: 'https://breezzetest.supabase.co',
+      publishableKey: 'sb_publishable_test',
+      client: MockClient((_) async {
+        requests++;
+        return http.Response('{}', 200);
+      }),
+    );
+    await expectLater(transport.signInWithIdToken(
+      provider: 'auth0', idToken: 'invalid-token',
+    ), throwsA(isA<SupabaseAuthTransportException>()));
+    expect(requests, 0);
+  });
+
+}

+import 'package:breezze/core/auth/auth_session.dart';
+import 'package:breezze/features/search/search_screen.dart';
+import 'package:breezze_design_system/breezze_design_system.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+import 'package:material_ui/material_ui.dart';
+
+class _SwitchableAuthSession extends AuthSessionController {
+  @override
+  AuthState build() =>
+      const AuthSignedIn(userId: 'alice', email: 'alice@example.test');
+
+  void switchTo(String userId) {
+    state = AuthSignedIn(userId: userId, email: '$userId@example.test');
+  }
+}
+
+void main() {
+  testWidgets('Search clears the prior listener query on account switch',
+      (tester) async {
+    final container = ProviderContainer(
+      overrides: [
+        authSessionProvider.overrideWith(_SwitchableAuthSession.new),
+      ],
+    );
+    addTearDown(container.dispose);
+
+    await tester.pumpWidget(
+      UncontrolledProviderScope(
+        container: container,
+        child: MaterialApp(
+          theme: bzLightTheme(),
+          home: const Scaffold(body: SearchScreen()),
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+
+    final inputFinder = find.byType(TextField).first;
+    await tester.enterText(inputFinder, 'A');
+    await tester.pump();
+    expect(tester.widget<TextField>(inputFinder).controller!.text, 'A');
+
+    (container.read(authSessionProvider.notifier) as _SwitchableAuthSession)
+        .switchTo('bob');
+    await tester.pump();
+
+    expect(tester.widget<TextField>(inputFinder).controller!.text, isEmpty);
+    expect(find.text('No results for "A"'), findsNothing);
+  });
+}

FILE apps/mobile/test/features/video/video_handoff_policy_test.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+import 'package:breezze/features/video/open_youtube_player.dart';
+import 'package:breezze_audio_contracts/audio_contracts.dart' show PlaybackState;
+import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart' show AppLifecycleState;
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+
+void main() {
+  bool canRestore({
+    bool wasPlaying = true,
+    String? openedEntryId = 'queue-entry-1',
+    String? currentEntryId = 'queue-entry-1',
+    String? openedTrackId = 'track-a',
+    String? currentTrackId = 'track-a',
+    PlaybackState state = PlaybackState.paused,
+    AppLifecycleState? lifecycle = AppLifecycleState.resumed,
+  }) =>
+      shouldResumeAudioAfterVideo(
+        wasPlaying: wasPlaying,
+        openedEntryId: openedEntryId,
+        openedTrackId: openedTrackId,
+        currentEntryId: currentEntryId,
+        currentTrackId: currentTrackId,
+        currentState: state,
+        lifecycleState: lifecycle,
+      );
+
+  test('resumes the exact paused queue entry after video closes', () {
+    expect(canRestore(), isTrue);
+  });
+
+  test('does not resume audio that was paused before video opened', () {
+    expect(canRestore(wasPlaying: false), isFalse);
+  });
+
+  test('does not resume a different queue entry selected during video', () {
+    expect(canRestore(currentEntryId: 'queue-entry-2'), isFalse);
+  });
+
+  test('does not resume a replacement recording in the same queue entry', () {
+    expect(canRestore(currentTrackId: 'track-b'), isFalse);
+  });
+
+  test('does not resume when the old queue or recording disappeared', () {
+    expect(canRestore(currentEntryId: null), isFalse);
+    expect(canRestore(openedTrackId: null), isFalse);
+  });
+
+  test('does not override a later play, stop or failed state', () {
+    expect(canRestore(state: PlaybackState.playing), isFalse);
+    expect(canRestore(state: PlaybackState.idle), isFalse);
+    expect(canRestore(state: PlaybackState.failed), isFalse);
+  });
+
+  test('does not resume while the application is backgrounded', () {
+    expect(canRestore(lifecycle: AppLifecycleState.paused), isFalse);
+    expect(canRestore(lifecycle: AppLifecycleState.inactive), isFalse);
+    expect(canRestore(lifecycle: null), isFalse);
+  });
+}

+  test('browser search history is isolated by account and survives re-open',
+      () async {
+    final values = <String, String>{};
+    Future<String?> read(String key) async => values[key];
+    Future<void> write(String key, String value) async {
+      values[key] = value;
+    }
+
+    SearchHistoryStore forOwner(String ownerId) => SearchHistoryStore(
+          ownerId: ownerId,
+          readBrowserValue: read,
+          writeBrowserValue: write,
+        );
+

+    expect(await forOwner('bob').load(), isEmpty);
+    await forOwner('bob').add('Amapiano');
+    expect(await forOwner('alice').load(), ['Bongo Flava']);
+    expect(await forOwner('bob').load(), ['Amapiano']);
+    await forOwner('alice').clear();
+    expect(await forOwner('alice').load(), isEmpty);
+    expect(await forOwner('bob').load(), ['Amapiano']);
+  });

FILE apps/mobile/tool/smoke_web_ui.cjs
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+// Browser-level smoke: verify the Flutter engine actually renders a view,
+// not only that the development server returns its bootstrap HTML.
+const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
+const fs = require('node:fs');
+const path = require('node:path');
+const { chromium } = require(path.join(
+  process.env.RUNNER_TEMP,
+  'breezze-browser',
+  'node_modules',
+  'playwright-core',
+));
+
+async function main() {
+  const browser = await chromium.launch({
+    executablePath: process.env.BREEZZE_BROWSER,
+    headless: true,
+    args: [
+      '--no-sandbox',
+      '--disable-dev-shm-usage',
+      '--use-angle=swiftshader',
+      '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader',
+    ],
+  });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage({
+      viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 },
+      deviceScaleFactor: 1,
+      reducedMotion: 'reduce',
+    });
+    const errors = [];
+    const consoleErrors = [];
+    const failedRequests = [];
+    page.on('pageerror', error => errors.push(error.message));
+    page.on('console', message => {
+      if (message.type() === 'error') consoleErrors.push(message.text());
+    });
+    page.on('requestfailed', request => {
+      failedRequests.push({
+        url: request.url(),
+        failure: request.failure()?.errorText,
+      });
+    });
+    const shot = path.join(process.env.RUNNER_TEMP, 'breezze-mobile-preview.png');
+    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:8080/', {
+      waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded',
+      timeout: 90000,
+    });
+
+    try {
+      await page.waitForFunction(
+        () => {
+          // Flutter paints inside the <flt-glass-pane> shadow root. A
+          // document.querySelector('canvas') misses that render surface.
+          const host = document.querySelector('flutter-view');
+          if (!host) return false;
+          function hasRenderedSurface(node) {
+            if (!node) return false;
+            if (node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE &&
+                node.matches('canvas, flt-scene-host, flt-picture')) {
+              return true;
+            }
+            if (node.shadowRoot && hasRenderedSurface(node.shadowRoot)) {
+              return true;
+            }
+            return Array.from(node.children ?? []).some(hasRenderedSurface);
+          }
+          return hasRenderedSurface(host);
+        },
+        null,
+        { timeout: 60000 },
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      const details = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+        title: document.title,
+        readyState: document.readyState,
+        body: document.body.innerHTML.slice(0, 4000),
+        canvasCount: document.querySelectorAll('canvas').length,
+        flutterHostCount: document.querySelectorAll('flutter-view, flt-glass-pane').length,
+        glassPaneShadow: document.querySelector('flt-glass-pane')?.shadowRoot?.innerHTML.slice(0, 1600) ?? null,
+        scriptCount: document.querySelectorAll('script').length,
+        scriptUrls: [...document.querySelectorAll('script')].slice(0, 10).map(s => s.src),
+      }));
+      console.error('Breezze browser DOM diagnostics:', JSON.stringify(details));
+      console.error('Breezze browser JS errors:', JSON.stringify(errors));
+      console.error('Breezze browser console errors:', JSON.stringify(consoleErrors));
+      console.error('Breezze browser failed requests:', JSON.stringify(failedRequests));
+      await page.screenshot({ path: shot, fullPage: true });
+      throw error;
+    }
+    await page.waitForTimeout(2500);
+    await page.screenshot({ path: shot, fullPage: true });
+    const size = fs.statSync(shot).size;
+    assert.ok(size > 5000, 'Browser screenshot is unexpectedly small');
+    assert.ok((await page.title()).includes('Breezze'), 'Incorrect page title');
+    assert.deepEqual(errors, [], 'Browser emitted uncaught JavaScript errors');
+
+    console.log('Flutter rendered in headless Chromium; mobile screenshot:', shot);
+    console.log('Screenshot size:', size, 'bytes');
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+  }
+}
+
+main().catch(error => {
+  console.error(error);
+  process.exitCode = 1;
+});

FILE apps/mobile/tool/test_listener_runtime.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
+device=${1:?pass the emulator or simulator ID}
+# Keep the generated test audio/video out of the shipping bundle.
+saved_manifest=$(mktemp)
+cp pubspec.yaml "$saved_manifest"
+trap 'cp "$saved_manifest" pubspec.yaml; rm -f "$saved_manifest"' EXIT
+sed '/^  assets:/a\
+    - integration_test/fixtures/
+' "$saved_manifest" > pubspec.yaml
+flutter pub get
+dart run build_runner build --delete-conflicting-outputs
+# Exercise the handoff race policy on both hosted platforms before launching native tests.
+flutter test test/features/video/video_handoff_policy_test.dart --reporter expanded
+live_args=()
+if [[ -n "${QA_PREVIEW_API_ORIGIN:-}" ]]; then
+  live_args+=(--dart-define=QA_LIVE_PREVIEW=true "--dart-define=BREEZZE_API_URL=$QA_PREVIEW_API_ORIGIN")
+fi
+if [[ "$device" != emulator-* ]]; then
+  # iOS simulator launch can hang after Xcode says "build done".
+  # Bound the actual test process and report a diagnostic rather than
+  # consuming the entire macOS runner allocation without useful logs.
+  set +e
+  python3 - "$device" "${live_args[@]}" <<'PY'
+import os
+import signal
+import subprocess
+import sys
+
+device, *extra = sys.argv[1:]
+command = [
+    'flutter', 'test', '-d', device,
+    'integration_test/listener_runtime_test.dart',
+    '--reporter', 'expanded', *extra,
+]
+print('QA_IOS_SIMULATOR_START ' + device, flush=True)
+proc = subprocess.Popen(command, start_new_session=True)
+try:
+    code = proc.wait(timeout=14 * 60)
+except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+    print('QA_IOS_SIMULATOR_TIMEOUT: flutter test did not finish in 14 minutes',
+          flush=True)
+    os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
+    try:
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+        os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
+        proc.wait()
+    sys.exit(124)
+sys.exit(code)
+PY
+  result=$?
+  set -e
+  if (( result != 0 )); then
+    echo "QA_IOS_SIMULATOR_FAILED with exit code $result"
+    xcrun simctl list devices booted || true
+  fi
+  exit "$result"
+fi
+
+log=$(mktemp)
+wait_for_marker() {
+  for ((i=0; i<900; i++)); do
+    if grep -q "$1" "$log"; then return; fi
+    if ! kill -0 "$test_pid" 2>/dev/null; then return 1; fi
+    sleep 1
+  done
+  return 1
+}
+# A hosted Android emulator occasionally fails to attach the Flutter Dart
+# Development Service after the APK has built and installed. Retry only that
+# infrastructure startup condition once; never retry/obscure app test failures.
+run_android_once() {
+  : > "$log"
+  flutter test -d "$device" integration_test/listener_runtime_test.dart --reporter expanded "${live_args[@]}" --dart-define=QA_ANDROID_MEDIA_KEYS=true >"$log" 2>&1 &
+  test_pid=$!
+  if wait_for_marker QA_ANDROID_MEDIA_KEYS_READY; then
+    adb -s "$device" shell input keyevent KEYCODE_HOME
+    sleep 1
+    adb -s "$device" shell dumpsys media_session | grep -F 'com.breezze.breezze'
+    adb -s "$device" shell cmd media_session dispatch pause
+    sleep 1
+    adb -s "$device" shell am start -n com.breezze.breezze/.MainActivity
+    if wait_for_marker QA_ANDROID_OS_PAUSED; then
+      adb -s "$device" shell cmd media_session dispatch play
+    fi
+  fi
+  local status=0
+  wait "$test_pid" || status=$?
+  cat "$log"
+  if (( status != 0 )) && grep -q 'Failed to start Dart Development Service' "$log"; then
+    return 75
+  fi
+  return "$status"
+}
+result=0
+run_android_once || result=$?
+if (( result == 75 )); then
+  echo "QA_ANDROID_DDS_STARTUP_RETRY: emulator was running but Flutter's debug service failed to attach."
+  adb -s "$device" wait-for-device
+  sleep 5
+  result=0
+  run_android_once || result=$?
+fi
+rm -f "$log"
+exit "$result"

FILE backend/api/prisma/migrations/20261123180000_supabase_auth_identity/migration.sql
@@ -0,0 +1,16 @@
+-- An explicit identity bridge permits Breezze sessions to coexist with
+-- Supabase Auth while users and data are migrated safely.
+-- Applied to whichever PostgreSQL/schema Prisma is configured to use;
+-- does not modify the unrelated paused Supabase project.
+CREATE TABLE "SupabaseAuthIdentity" (
+  "userId" TEXT NOT NULL,
+  "subject" TEXT NOT NULL,
+  "linkedAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
+  CONSTRAINT "SupabaseAuthIdentity_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("userId")
+);
+CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "SupabaseAuthIdentity_subject_key"
+  ON "SupabaseAuthIdentity"("subject");
+ALTER TABLE "SupabaseAuthIdentity" ADD CONSTRAINT
+  "SupabaseAuthIdentity_userId_fkey"
+  FOREIGN KEY ("userId") REFERENCES "User"("id")
+  ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE;

@@ -429,6 +432,51 @@ export class AuthService {
     });
   }
 
+  /** Invalidate every active refresh-token family owned by this user.
+   * JWT strategy checks active session families, so already-issued access
+   * tokens stop working on the next authenticated API request.
+   */
+  async changePassword(
+    userId: string,
+    currentPassword: string,
+    newPassword: string,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    if (newPassword.length < 12 || newPassword.length > 128) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('PASSWORD_POLICY');
+    }
+    if (currentPassword === newPassword) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('PASSWORD_REUSED');
+    }
+    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({
+      where: { id: userId },
+      select: { passwordHash: true },
+    });
+    if (!user || !await argon2.verify(user.passwordHash, currentPassword).catch(() => false)) {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('INVALID_CREDENTIALS');
+    }
+    const passwordHash = await argon2.hash(newPassword, passwordHashOptions);
+    // Optimistic guard prevents two simultaneous changes accepting a stale
+    // current password. Revoke all device sessions in the same transaction.
+    await this.prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
+      const updated = await tx.user.updateMany({
+        where: { id: userId, passwordHash: user.passwordHash },
+        data: { passwordHash },
+      });
+      if (updated.count !== 1) throw new UnauthorizedException('PASSWORD_CHANGED');
+      await tx.refreshToken.updateMany({
+        where: { userId, revokedAt: null },
+        data: { revokedAt: new Date() },
+      });
+    });
+  }
+
+  async revokeAllSessions(userId: string): Promise<void> {
+    await this.prisma.refreshToken.updateMany({
+      where: { userId, revokedAt: null },
+      data: { revokedAt: new Date() },
+    });
+  }
+

+import {
+  ConflictException,
+  ServiceUnavailableException,
+  UnauthorizedException,
+} from '@nestjs/common';

+const token = `${'a'.repeat(50)}.${'b'.repeat(60)}.${'c'.repeat(70)}`;
+const supabaseUser = {
+  id: '123e4567-e89b-42d3-a456-426614174000',
+  email: 'listener@example.test',
+  email_confirmed_at: '2026-11-23T00:00:00Z',

+describe('SupabaseAuthBridge (private NestJS identity exchange)', () => {

+  const oldKey = process.env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
+  const oldClaim = process.env.SUPABASE_LEGACY_CLAIM_ENABLED;

+  let auth: any;

+      supabaseAuthIdentity: {
+        findUnique: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
+        create: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ userId: 'breezze-1' }),

+      user: {
+        findFirst: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
+        findUnique: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({
+          id: 'breezze-1', email: 'listener@example.test',

+        create: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ id: 'breezze-1' }),
+      },
+    };
+    auth = { issueVerifiedSocialSession: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({
+      accessToken: 'breezze-session', refreshToken: 'opaque-refresh',
+      user: { id: 'breezze-1', email: 'listener@example.test' },
+    }) };
+    bridge = new SupabaseAuthBridge(prisma as PrismaService, auth as AuthService);
+    process.env.SUPABASE_LEGACY_CLAIM_ENABLED = 'true';
+    fetchSpy = jest.spyOn(global, 'fetch').mockResolvedValue(
+      new Response(JSON.stringify(supabaseUser), { status: 200 }),
+    );
+  });
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    fetchSpy.mockRestore();
+    if (oldUrl === undefined) delete process.env.SUPABASE_URL;
+    else process.env.SUPABASE_URL = oldUrl;
+    if (oldKey === undefined) delete process.env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
+    else process.env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = oldKey;
+    if (oldClaim === undefined) delete process.env.SUPABASE_LEGACY_CLAIM_ENABLED;
+    else process.env.SUPABASE_LEGACY_CLAIM_ENABLED = oldClaim;
+  });
+
+  it('verifies a Supabase user via /auth/v1/user before issuing a Breezze session', async () => {
+    await expect(bridge.exchange(token)).resolves.toMatchObject({
+      accessToken: 'breezze-session',
+    });
+    expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    const [url, options] = fetchSpy.mock.calls[0];
+    expect(url).toBe('https://breezzetest.supabase.co/auth/v1/user');
+    expect(options.redirect).toBe('manual');
+    expect(options.headers.apikey).toBe('sb_publishable_test-only');
+    expect(options.headers.Authorization).toBe(`Bearer ${token}`);
+    expect(prisma.user.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({

+        passwordHash: expect.stringMatching(/^!supabase-managed:/),
+        supabaseIdentity: { create: { subject: supabaseUser.id } },

+  });
+
+  it('refuses unverified emails even if the provider returns a user ID', async () => {

+      ...supabaseUser, email_confirmed_at: null,
+    }), { status: 200 }));
+    await expect(bridge.exchange(token)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedException);
+    expect(prisma.user.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('never silently links a pre-existing account by matching email', async () => {
+    prisma.user.findFirst.mockResolvedValue({ id: 'old-account' });
+    await expect(bridge.exchange(token)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictException);
+    expect(prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+    expect(prisma.user.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('reuses only a stored, explicitly linked Supabase subject', async () => {
+    prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.findUnique.mockResolvedValue({ userId: 'owner-1' });

+    expect(prisma.user.findFirst).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('explicit linking requires the existing account and verified Supabase email to match', async () => {
+    await expect(bridge.link('breezze-1', token)).resolves.toEqual({ linked: true });
+    expect(prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
+      data: { userId: 'breezze-1', subject: supabaseUser.id },
+    });
+    prisma.user.findUnique.mockResolvedValue({ id: 'breezze-1', email: 'other@example.test' });

+      .rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedException);
+  });
+
+  it('rejects linking a Supabase subject already owned by another listener', async () => {
+    prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.findUnique.mockResolvedValue({ userId: 'other-account' });
+    await expect(bridge.link('breezze-1', token))
+      .rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ConflictException);
+  });
+
+  it('fails closed when Supabase rejects a user token', async () => {
+    fetchSpy.mockResolvedValue(new Response('{}', { status: 401 }));
+    await expect(bridge.exchange(token)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedException);
+    expect(auth.issueVerifiedSocialSession).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('never falls back to an untrusted host, missing project, or broken provider', async () => {
+    process.env.SUPABASE_URL = 'https://attacker.example';
+    await expect(bridge.exchange(token)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ServiceUnavailableException);

+    await expect(bridge.exchange(token)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ServiceUnavailableException);
+  });

+      data:{ userId:'existing-user', subject:supabaseUser.id },

+      .rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedException);

+    await expect(bridge.claimLegacy(token, supabaseUser.email, 'password'))
+      .rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ServiceUnavailableException);
+    expect(fetchSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });

FILE backend/api/src/auth/supabase-auth.bridge.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,221 @@
+import {
+  ConflictException,
+  Injectable,
+  ServiceUnavailableException,
+  UnauthorizedException,
+} from '@nestjs/common';
+import { randomBytes } from 'crypto';
+import * as argon2 from 'argon2';
+import { PrismaService } from '../prisma.service';
+import { AuthService } from './auth.service';
+
+interface VerifiedSupabaseUser {
+  id: string;
+  email: string;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Staged Supabase Auth bridge.
+ *
+ * The remote Supabase Auth server validates the user's *access JWT*;
+ * its publishable key identifies the app but does not authenticate a user.
+ * Breezze's existing session family and authorization rules remain intact.
+ * Existing accounts require an authenticated explicit link, never an
+ * email-match auto-link.
+ */
+@Injectable()
+export class SupabaseAuthBridge {
+  constructor(
+    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
+    private readonly auth: AuthService,
+  ) {}
+
+  private config(): { origin: string; publishableKey: string } {
+    const rawUrl = process.env.SUPABASE_URL?.trim();
+    const publishableKey = process.env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY?.trim();
+    if (!rawUrl || !publishableKey) {
+      throw new ServiceUnavailableException('SUPABASE_AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED');
+    }
+    let url: URL;
+    try {
+      url = new URL(rawUrl);
+    } catch {
+      throw new ServiceUnavailableException('SUPABASE_AUTH_URL_INVALID');
+    }
+    if (url.protocol !== 'https:' ||
+        !/^[a-z0-9-]+\.supabase\.co$/.test(url.hostname) ||
+        url.pathname !== '/' || url.search || url.hash ||
+        url.username || url.password || url.port) {
+      throw new ServiceUnavailableException('SUPABASE_AUTH_URL_INVALID');
+    }
+    return { origin: url.origin, publishableKey };
+  }
+
+  async verifyAccessToken(accessToken: string): Promise<VerifiedSupabaseUser> {
+    if (typeof accessToken !== 'string' ||
+        accessToken.length < 40 || accessToken.length > 8192 ||
+        !/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$/.test(accessToken) ||
+        accessToken.split('.').length !== 3) {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('SUPABASE_TOKEN_INVALID');
+    }
+    const config = this.config();
+    let response: Response;
+    try {
+      response = await fetch(config.origin + '/auth/v1/user', {
+        method: 'GET',
+        redirect: 'manual',
+        headers: {
+          apikey: config.publishableKey,
+          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
+        },
+        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(7000),
+      });
+    } catch {
+      throw new ServiceUnavailableException('SUPABASE_AUTH_UNAVAILABLE');
+    }
+    if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('SUPABASE_TOKEN_REJECTED');
+    }
+    if (response.status !== 200) {
+      throw new ServiceUnavailableException('SUPABASE_AUTH_UNAVAILABLE');
+    }
+    let user: Record<string, unknown>;
+    try {
+      user = await response.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
+    } catch {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('SUPABASE_USER_INVALID');
+    }
+    if (!user || typeof user.id !== 'string' ||
+        !/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(user.id) ||
+        typeof user.email !== 'string' ||
+        !/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(user.email)) {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('SUPABASE_USER_INVALID');
+    }
+    // Prevent unverified Supabase emails from bypassing Breezze's account
+    // requirements, including OAuth identities with no verified email.
+    if (typeof user.email_confirmed_at !== 'string' ||
+        !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(user.email_confirmed_at))) {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('SUPABASE_EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED');
+    }
+    return { id: user.id, email: user.email.trim().toLowerCase() };
+  }
+
+  async exchange(accessToken: string) {
+    const identity = await this.verifyAccessToken(accessToken);
+    const linked = await this.prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.findUnique({
+      where: { subject: identity.id },
+      select: { userId: true },
+    });
+    if (linked) {
+      return this.auth.issueVerifiedSocialSession(linked.userId);
+    }
+
+    // Matching email is not evidence of ownership of a pre-existing Breezze
+    // account; force an explicit account-link operation with its own session.
+    const existing = await this.prisma.user.findFirst({
+      where: { email: { equals: identity.email, mode: 'insensitive' } },
+      select: { id: true },
+    });
+    if (existing) throw new ConflictException('SUPABASE_ACCOUNT_LINK_REQUIRED');
+
+    let userId: string;
+    try {
+      const newUser = await this.prisma.user.create({
+        data: {
+          email: identity.email,
+          displayName: null,
+          // Not an Argon2 hash: intentionally impossible to use as a local
+          // password; Supabase Auth owns the primary authentication method.
+          passwordHash: '!supabase-managed:' + randomBytes(32).toString('hex'),
+          supabaseIdentity: { create: { subject: identity.id } },
+        },
+        select: { id: true },
+      });
+      userId = newUser.id;
+    } catch (error) {
+      // Two devices can exchange the same newly registered identity. Accept
+      // only the identity-matching winner, never a competing email owner.
+      if ((error as { code?: string })?.code !== 'P2002') throw error;
+      const winner = await this.prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.findUnique({
+        where: { subject: identity.id },
+        select: { userId: true },
+      });
+      if (!winner) throw new ConflictException('SUPABASE_ACCOUNT_LINK_REQUIRED');
+      userId = winner.userId;
+    }
+    return this.auth.issueVerifiedSocialSession(userId);
+  }
+
+
+  /** Explicit migration claim requires verified Supabase identity and old
+   * Breezze password. Never merge preexisting accounts by matching email. */
+  async claimLegacy(accessToken: string, email: string, legacyPassword: string) {
+    if (process.env.SUPABASE_LEGACY_CLAIM_ENABLED !== 'true') {
+      throw new ServiceUnavailableException('SUPABASE_ACCOUNT_MIGRATION_DISABLED');
+    }
+    const identity = await this.verifyAccessToken(accessToken);
+    if (identity.email !== email.trim().toLowerCase()) {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('INVALID_CREDENTIALS');
+    }
+    const owner = await this.prisma.user.findFirst({
+      where: { email: { equals: identity.email, mode: 'insensitive' } },
+      select: { id: true, passwordHash: true },
+    });
+    if (!owner?.passwordHash.startsWith('$argon2id$')) {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('INVALID_CREDENTIALS');
+    }
+    let verified = false;
+    try {
+      verified = await argon2.verify(owner.passwordHash, legacyPassword);
+    } catch (_) {
+      verified = false;
+    }
+    if (!verified) throw new UnauthorizedException('INVALID_CREDENTIALS');
+    try {
+      await this.prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.create({
+        data: { userId: owner.id, subject: identity.id },
+      });
+    } catch (error) {
+      if ((error as { code?: string })?.code !== 'P2002') throw error;
+      const winner = await this.prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.findUnique({
+        where: { subject: identity.id }, select: { userId: true },
+      });
+      if (winner?.userId !== owner.id) {
+        throw new ConflictException('SUPABASE_IDENTITY_IN_USE');
+      }
+    }
+    return this.auth.issueVerifiedSocialSession(owner.id);
+  }
+
+  async link(userId: string, accessToken: string) {
+    const identity = await this.verifyAccessToken(accessToken);
+    const owner = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({
+      where: { id: userId },
+      select: { id: true, email: true },
+    });
+    if (!owner || owner.email.toLowerCase() !== identity.email) {
+      throw new UnauthorizedException('SUPABASE_IDENTITY_EMAIL_MISMATCH');
+    }
+    const linked = await this.prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.findUnique({
+      where: { subject: identity.id },
+      select: { userId: true },
+    });
+    if (linked) {
+      if (linked.userId !== userId) {
+        throw new ConflictException('SUPABASE_IDENTITY_IN_USE');
+      }
+      return { linked: true };
+    }
+    try {
+      await this.prisma.supabaseAuthIdentity.create({
+        data: { userId, subject: identity.id },
+      });
+    } catch (error) {
+      if ((error as { code?: string })?.code === 'P2002') {
+        throw new ConflictException('SUPABASE_IDENTITY_IN_USE');
+      }
+      throw error;
+    }
+    return { linked: true };
+  }
+}

FILE backend/api/src/auth/supabase-auth.controller.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,61 @@
+import {
+  Body,
+  Controller,
+  Header,
+  HttpCode,
+  HttpStatus,
+  Post,
+  UseGuards,
+} from '@nestjs/common';
+import { IsEmail, IsJWT, IsString, MaxLength, MinLength } from 'class-validator';
+import { CurrentUser } from './current-user.decorator';
+import { JwtAuthGuard } from './jwt-auth.guard';
+import { SupabaseAuthBridge } from './supabase-auth.bridge';
+
+class SupabaseExchangeDto {
+  @IsString()
+  @IsJWT()
+  accessToken!: string;
+}
+
+class SupabaseClaimDto extends SupabaseExchangeDto {
+  @IsEmail()
+  @MaxLength(254)
+  email!: string;
+
+  @IsString()
+  @MinLength(1)
+  @MaxLength(128)
+  legacyPassword!: string;
+}
+
+@Controller('auth/supabase')
+export class SupabaseAuthController {
+  constructor(private readonly bridge: SupabaseAuthBridge) {}
+
+  // Supabase already validated credentials; verify its current access
+  // token server-side before minting a Breezze session family.
+  @Post('exchange')
+  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
+  @Header('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store')
+  exchange(@Body() dto: SupabaseExchangeDto) {
+    return this.bridge.exchange(dto.accessToken);
+  }
+
+  @Post('claim')
+  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
+  @Header('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store')
+  claim(@Body() dto: SupabaseClaimDto) {
+    return this.bridge.claimLegacy(dto.accessToken, dto.email, dto.legacyPassword);
+  }
+
+  // Existing Breezze password users must prove *both* identities before
+  // linking. There is no silent email-based account takeover.
+  @Post('link')
+  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
+  @Header('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store')
+  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
+  link(@CurrentUser() userId: string, @Body() dto: SupabaseExchangeDto) {
+    return this.bridge.link(userId, dto.accessToken);
+  }
+}

     expect(response.setHeader).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Retry-After', '60');
   });
