+// Run only against the disposable Postgres and media worker on the CI runner.
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { randomBytes, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
+import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
+
+assert.equal(new URL(process.env.DATABASE_URL).hostname, '127.0.0.1');
+assert.equal(process.env.CDN_BASE_URL, 'http://127.0.0.1:8787');
+const db = new PrismaClient();
+const id = '24ead000-7bce-498c-806c-d6084a0ed108';
+const audio = await readFile(new URL('../../../apps/mobile/integration_test/fixtures/tone.m4a', import.meta.url));
+let token;
+async function api(path, body) {
+  const response = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/v1' + path, {
+    method: body === undefined ? 'GET' : 'POST',
+    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-breezze-country': 'TZ',
+      ...(token ? { authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } : {}) },
+    body: JSON.stringify(body)
+  });
+  assert.ok(response.ok, path + ' returned ' + response.status);
+  return (await response.json()).data;
+}
+try {
+  await db.track.create({ data: { id, title: 'Runtime recording', duration: 40,
+    artist: { create: { name: 'Breezze QA' } }, source: 'breezze_internal',
+    territories: { create: { territory: 'TZ' } },
+    rights: { create: [{action:'stream.full'}, {action:'download'}] } } });
+  const user = await api('/auth/register', { email: randomUUID() + '@example.invalid', password: randomBytes(24).toString('hex') });
+  token = user.accessToken;
+  assert.ok(token);
+  const session = await api('/playback/sessions', {trackId:id});
+  assert.equal(session.playback.previewOnly, false);
+  const range = await fetch(session.playback.streamUrl, { headers: { Range: 'bytes=0-127' } });
+  assert.equal(range.status, 206);
+  assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(await range.arrayBuffer()), audio.subarray(0,128));
+  const head = await fetch(session.playback.streamUrl, {method: 'HEAD'});
+  assert.equal(head.status, 200);
+  const etag = head.headers.get('etag');
+  assert.ok(/^"[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,120}"$/.test(etag || ''), 'R2 must expose a strong version');
+  const matchingRange = await fetch(session.playback.streamUrl, {
+    headers: { Range: 'bytes=128-255', 'If-Range': etag },
+  });
+  assert.equal(matchingRange.status, 206);
+  assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(await matchingRange.arrayBuffer()), audio.subarray(128,256));
+  const changedMaster = await fetch(session.playback.streamUrl, {
+    headers: { Range: 'bytes=128-255', 'If-Range': '"obsolete-r2-object"' },
+  });
+  assert.equal(changedMaster.status, 200, 'mismatched If-Range must force a full replacement');
+  assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(await changedMaster.arrayBuffer()), audio);
+  const stream = await fetch(session.playback.streamUrl);
+  assert.equal(stream.status, 200);
+  assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(await stream.arrayBuffer()), audio);
+  await api('/playback/sessions/' + session.id + '/pause', {});
+  await api('/playback/sessions/' + session.id + '/resume', {});
+  await api('/playback/sessions/' + session.id + '/stop', {});
+  const grant = await api('/downloads', {trackId:id});
+  assert.equal(grant.allowed,true);
+  assert.ok(Date.parse(grant.offlineLeaseExpiresAt) > Date.now());
+  const saved = await fetch(grant.url);
+  assert.equal(saved.status, 200);
+  assert.deepEqual(Buffer.from(await saved.arrayBuffer()),audio);
+  assert.equal((await api('/downloads/' + id + '/revalidate', {})).allowed,true);
+  await db.rightsUsagePermission.delete({where:{trackId_action:{trackId:id,action:'download'}}});
+  assert.equal((await api('/downloads/' + id + '/revalidate', {})).allowed,false);
+  console.log('Verified full recording, ETag/If-Range safety, range, pause/resume/stop, download, lease renewal and revocation');
+} finally {
+  await db.$disconnect();
+}

 # Breezze Product Experience Plan
 
+> **Current release scope:** The audio-first execution plan supersedes older
+> aspirational sections below. The live client has **four** destinations:
+> **Home, Browse, Library, Search**. Radio, an artist portal, billing,
+> subscriptions and social features are not release requirements.
+> Catalog metadata is not a music license. See
+> [the verification register](audit/RELEASE_EXECUTION_STATUS.md) for
+> independently verified functionality and outstanding blockers.
+
 This specification applies the supplied music-app feature inventory to Breezze's independent product identity and the existing Flutter-first architecture. It describes target behavior; a listed capability is not considered shipped until its real data source, persistence, permissions, and tests exist.

FILE docs/architecture/BREEZZE_AUDIO_FIRST_EXECUTION_AND_ACCEPTANCE.md
@@ -0,0 +1,138 @@
+# Breezze — audio-first execution and acceptance contract
+
+**Canonical repository:** [MetroKim/Breezze](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze)  
+**Legacy repository:** [MetroKim/MetroMusic](https://github.com/MetroKim/MetroMusic)  
+**Status:** staged implementation plan and verified baseline, **not** a production-release certificate.  
+**Reconciled:** 2026-11-23.
+
+This contract translates the product's ten-phase audio-first plan into actions compatible with the code that actually exists. Do not replace functioning infrastructure merely because another package was listed in an exploratory proposal.
+
+## Repository consolidation and change control
+
+- The Breezze main branch already includes the MetroMusic application history, including shared commit `a13d3f29d26f68527de6bd45f5c7bc2d05cf3fe3`, plus later Flutter audio/video integration. `MetroMusic/main` adds only a README redirect after that shared commit; it is **not** another active application to copy.
+- All new application work targets `MetroKim/Breezze`. Keep MetroMusic read-only as a legacy reference until GitHub's repository administrator archives it; archival or deletion is **not** implied by this document.
+- Old feature branches in MetroMusic may diverge and must be evaluated file-by-file; never mass-merge historical branches, duplicate native apps, or overwrite generated runners.
+- Preserve `apps/mobile` as the shipping Flutter project, `backend/api` as the NestJS API, `backend/ytmusicapi` as a discovery service, and `packages/*` as the shared implementation.
+- The existing draft [playback verification PR #3](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze/pull/3) has a green regression baseline at `391dcd296fe995c3f3499337947747485294b317`. Its code is not yet on `main`. Any later change needs CI against its **new exact commit**.
+
+## Product contract
+
+Four top-level destinations: **Home, Browse, Library, Search**. One application-owned queue and mini/full player shared between routes. No Radio destination, ads, subscriptions, paid tier, artist portal or paywall. Listener-facing artist, album, playlist and account pages are allowed.
+
+### One audio engine; video is opt-in
+
+```text
+Flutter UI (Home / Browse / Library / Search / Now Playing)
+   -> Riverpod PlayerController (one queue, one source of truth)
+       -> backend playback authorization + source resolver
+       -> just_audio adapter (ExoPlayer / AVFoundation)
+       -> audio_service (system controls) + audio_session (focus/interruption)
+
+Video action (separate, explicit UI)
+   -> pause current player/entry
+   -> single video_player decoder OR official in-app video embed
+   -> wait for decoder route disposal
+   -> resume only the same paused entry, same recording, app foreground
+```
+
+Keep the existing `just_audio`, `audio_service`, `audio_session` and `video_player` stack. The app already has a global `PlayerController`, a platform bridge and a video handoff flow. `just_audio_background` is not required alongside the custom `audio_service` handler. A generic media-kit video pool is not a prerequisite for one opt-in video surface. Desktop audio plugins do not imply a second independent global player.
+
+**Audio handoff invariants:** do not reopen a video's decoder while an unrelated track was selected during the asynchronous pause; do not auto-resume a replaced queue entry, a different recording, a user-paused player or a backgrounded app. These behaviors are covered by `video_handoff_policy_test.dart` and by emulator runtime integration tests for the baseline flow.
+
+## Playback sources: rights boundary, not just network reachability
+
+| Source | Discovery | Native full recording | Preview | Offline |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| YouTube Music metadata via `ytmusicapi` | Best effort | No grant from metadata alone | Only separately authorized source | No |
+| Deezer public API | Metadata | No automatic full-catalog entitlement; backend may use a separately documented authorized recording | Provider-issued short preview, labelled as such | No preview download |
+| Audius official API | Public/eligible tracks | Eligible public, ungated API-accessible recordings only | Where permitted | Only if separately authorized |
+| Breezze-owned or explicitly licensed master | Yes | Rights + territory + expiring stream grant required | If allowed | Signed download + bounded offline lease |
+| User-chosen local file | Local | Yes | N/A | Already local |
+
+An ARL cookie, YouTube watch URL, extraction tool or third-party preview is **not** proof of a full-stream/download licence. Do not implement cookie-based access-control circumvention or silently substitute a different master. Failover must verify the *same recording*, territory, allowed action, session and expiry. Prefer ISRC plus edition/artist/duration verification; ambiguous candidates produce an unavailable state.
+
+## Ten implementation phases with real acceptance gates
+
+### 1. Foundation and canonical repo — largely in place
+
+- [x] Central Flutter monorepo, shared packages, NestJS API, generated platform targets and cross-platform CI.
+- [x] One canonical Breezze repository with the previous project history.
+- [ ] Administratively archive old MetroMusic after checking all active integrations/remotes; keep redirect and history.
+- [ ] Audit app store metadata, app icon, splash, package identifiers and hosted deployment references for remaining MetroMusic names.
+
+### 2. Brand and adaptive theme — partial
+
+- [x] Shared design-system tokens and Breezze-specific branded UI foundations exist.
+- [x] Wire the bundled launcher icon to palette extraction and use the contrast-checked seeded accent; preserve monochrome for achromatic artwork. Added adaptive theme unit coverage.
+- [x] Extract per-song artwork palettes for the Now Playing ambient backdrop, with race guards and muted tint; use high-contrast and monochrome fallback for unsaturated art.
+- [ ] Audit high-contrast/reduced-motion themes and asset variants on physical supported devices.
+- [ ] Test dark/light/system/high-contrast themes, dynamic type, accessible focus and reduced motion on supported platforms.
+
+### 3. Listener shell — partial
+
+- [x] Four-destination Flutter navigation and shared player foundation.
+- [ ] Review every screen in device-width and desktop layouts: Home, Browse, Library, Search, mini-player, Now Playing, queue, album/artist/playlist pages. Verify pressed/hero/blur behavior and empty/error/offline states.
+- [ ] Confirm a single player survives route changes, sign-out, account switching and lifecycle events.
+
+### 4. Backend and provider integration — partial
+
+- [x] NestJS, Prisma/PostgreSQL, caching foundations, authenticated session resolution, metadata providers and permission checks exist.
+- [ ] Map each visible catalog CTA to a tested backend route. Preserve provider attribution and distinguish metadata-only, full-stream and preview outcomes.
+- [ ] Add provider budget, timeout, outage, source-identity and rights-denial tests. Validate production configuration separately from local mocks.
+- [ ] Use independently authorized licensed sources instead of assuming a Deezer ARL can supply every full recording.
+
+### 5. Playback and offline — emulator baseline verified
+
+- [x] Android and iOS emulators verified local download, offline audio decode, pause/seek/resume, video handoff and native Deezer **preview** decode at PR #3 head; Android also exercised OS media pause/play.
+- [x] Test backend with disposable licensed fixture: byte-range full stream, session controls, download grant, lease revalidation and revocation.
+- [x] Implement device-owned local-file native gapless playlists (Android/iOS/macOS) with one just_audio decoder, native index updates and safe queue-edit fallback; cached catalog downloads are excluded until every offline lease can be validated. Unit and emulator tests added, **exact-head CI pending**.
+- [ ] Measure actual audible silence at two adjacent local-track boundaries and validate long playlist/battery behavior on real devices.
+- [ ] For full remote tracks, implement queue grants with individually validated licensing/session lifecycle before enabling native gapless; keep current per-track backend resolution until proven safe.
+- [ ] Verify skip/repeat/shuffle transitions, failed source retry, network changes and grant-expiry recovery.
+- [ ] Test physical lock-screen, headset/Bluetooth, phone call, and audio-focus behavior. Emulators cannot certify these.
+- [ ] Do not claim gapless merely because `just_audio` has playlist APIs; time the end-to-start output gap with independently granted sources.
+
+### 6. Optional native DSP — not enabled
+
+- [ ] Benchmark output EQ/crossfade/spatial needs before installing native FFI libraries.
+- [ ] Pick one native PCM/output owner and a well-defined integration path. A second FFI player cannot simply be attached to `just_audio` output; uncoordinated engines cause double playback.
+- [ ] Real-time callback contract: no allocation, locks, I/O or Dart VM entry. Measure latency, CPU, battery and ten minutes of glitch-free audio on low-end physical Android.
+
+### 7. Cache and offline hierarchy — partial
+
+- [x] Local downloads with rights/owner metadata and backend lease checks have automated coverage.
+- [x] Fail closed on invalid, expired or preview-only download grants; require HTTPS and a bounded offline lease, and reject downloads after account switches.
+- [x] Bind partial and finalized audio to a stable server assetVersion or the media Worker's strong R2 ETag; use If-Range and validate all 206 responses. Revalidate completed unversioned-backend assets by authorized HEAD, and fail closed or restart on version mismatch.
+- [x] Deny playback of legacy downloaded files whose offline lease is missing or expired. Retain their metadata so an eligible listener can renew rights when online.
+- [ ] Add **authorized** metadata/source prefetch for up to three queue entries; key by listener, territory, recording edition, quality and rights expiry. The signed R2 gateway now exposes ETags; a server-issued encoded-asset manifest/version remains desirable for stronger production integrity validation.
+- [ ] Never persist unauthorized provider audio or share signed user-specific URLs in edge caches. Validate range resumption, evictions, corrupted assets, revocations, airplane-mode behavior and account isolation.
+- [ ] Do not claim arbitrary streamed audio is offline-playable after half its bytes arrive: codec/container boundaries, explicit caching rights and verified chunk indexes are prerequisites.
+
+### 8. Optional video — single native controller in place
+
+- [x] `video_player` supports an explicit local MP4 video surface; the official embedded player handles compatible provider videos separately.
+- [ ] Keep at most one active native video decoder for the current app. Add a bounded LRU pool **only if** a multi-video feed becomes an approved product requirement and hardware profiling justifies it.
+- [ ] Test 20-video scroll and decoder budgets on budget hardware only if a feed is actually shipped.
+
+### 9. Audio/video coordination — guarded regression work
+
+- [x] Audio pauses for video and resumes after the video route completes and releases its decoder; Android/iOS emulator smoke tests pass on baseline.
+- [x] Add race guard for a new audio selection while handoff pause is pending, with unit coverage for queue/recording/lifecycle mismatch.
+- [ ] Add 50-switch stress test with memory/decoder/focus instrumentation; include app background/foreground, video error, OS interruption and explicit user pause cases.
+- [ ] Never transfer timestamps between different recordings or mismatched video edits without a verified timeline mapping.
+
+### 10. Lyrics, operations and release — partial
+
+- [x] Lyrics route and seeking foundations exist in the app.
+- [ ] Choose a tested parser for ordinary/timed/word-synced lyrics; only claim word-level highlighting when licensed provider timestamps are available.
+- [ ] Add accessibility, localization, reduced motion, privacy-safe telemetry, crash monitoring, deployment smoke tests and reproducible backup/restore.
+- [ ] Release only after real-device testing, source rights approval, deployment checks and signing. CI green does not imply production readiness.
+
+## Baseline evidence and repeatable checks
+
+The prior baseline on PR #3 passed:
+- [CI run 45144191636](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze/actions/runs/45144191636): Flutter tests/analyzer, NestJS/Prisma/Postgres tests, Cloudflare Worker checks, Android/iOS/Linux/macOS/Windows/Web builds.
+- [Listener runtime 45144191610](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze/actions/runs/45144191610): Android emulator, iPhone simulator and local Postgres/R2 licensed-media fixture.
+- [CodeQL 45144191623](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze/actions/runs/45144191623): JS/TS, Actions, Python, Java/Kotlin.
+
+Each new commit should run `flutter analyze`, Flutter app/package tests, backend tests and applicable platform builds in GitHub Actions; run native decoder tests on Android/iOS simulators for handoff changes. Report failing, skipped and pending jobs explicitly. This document records an **execution plan**, not ten completed phases.

FILE docs/audit/RELEASE_EXECUTION_STATUS.md
@@ -0,0 +1,128 @@
+# Breezze — Fullstack Release Execution Status
+
+This is the **current evidence-based acceptance board**, replacing assumptions
+from older plans and archived MetroMusic documentation.
+
+## Source of truth
+
+- Canonical product repository: `MetroKim/Breezze`, not `MetroKim/MetroMusic`.
+- Current release: free, listener-first, **audio-first**, with Home, Browse,
+  Library and Search as the four destinations; settings and Now Playing are
+  secondary destinations.
+- One `PlayerController` and shared queue. No second catalog player per tab.
+- Radio, artist portal, subscriptions, paywalls, billing, and social features
+  are explicitly deferred; related legacy modules do not imply shipping UI.
+- YouTube Music metadata is not a playback entitlement. Deezer API previews
+  are limited previews, not full commercial-catalog grants. Only user-owned
+  local media or rights-authorized provider/catalog assets may play or download.
+
+## Verified in source and automated checks
+
+| Workstream | Evidence | Release status |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| Flutter application structure | `apps/mobile/pubspec.yaml`, `lib/`, Linux runner, `run.sh` | Present; end-to-end UI journeys still need testing |
+| Four-tab UI and global mini-player | `apps/mobile/lib/widgets/bz_shell.dart` | Implemented; device accessibility audit remains |
+| Search, Browse and Library | Feature screens + backend catalog adapters | Implemented for supported sources; provider/data completeness not certified |
+| Accessible filters and player seek | `bz_filter_pill.dart`, `bz_playback_scrubber.dart` and widget tests | Changed; acceptance requires green CI at exact commit |
+| Authentication/account isolation | Auth guards, scoped local data tests, backend integration specs | Automated foundations present; cross-tenant release audit incomplete |
+| Shared playback & OS focus | PlayerController, engine adapter, background audio bridge | Automated tests; device/route and lock-screen hardware gates open |
+| Authenticated backend | `backend/api` NestJS + Prisma, unit/integration tests | CI testable; live production environment not certified |
+| Storage and CDN signing | `infra/cloudflare` workers and licensed-media fixture | CI testable; external licensing/deployment required |
+| Offline download lease/version rules | PR #4, `feat/audio-first-video-handoff` | Draft; must merge and pass tests before declaring shipped |
+| Linux/Android/Web/Windows/macOS/iOS | `.github/workflows/breezze-quality.yml` | Compilation jobs exist; physical-device acceptance open |
+| Browser launch | `.github/workflows/web-preview-smoke.yml` | HTTP server/bootstrap smoke passed; full interactive browser coverage open |
+
+## Audit results and active remediation
+
+1. Mainline playback UI: tapping the mini-player progress strip previously
+   updated only a transient drag state without seeking. The shared scrubber
+   now commits taps and drags and exposes screen-reader increment/decrement
+   actions.
+2. Filter controls: Browse, Library and Search each duplicated a 36dp
+   gesture-only pill. They now share `BzFilterPill` with a minimum 48dp
+   touch target, keyboard interaction via `InkWell`, proper selection
+   semantics, and reduced-motion behavior.
+3. Tab long-press navigation: selection previously used the entire screen's
+   width and hardcoded horizontal offsets. It now computes the hit location
+   in the actual tab-bar render box.
+4. Search: provider failures now expose a visible in-app retry action;
+   empty results show a clear-search recovery action.
+5. PR #4 API client failure: unnecessary Dart null assertion in
+   `packages/api_client/lib/src/downloads_library.dart` corrected.
+6. Android CodeQL build failure: Maven Central returned HTTP 429 while
+   downloading a Kotlin Gradle plugin. This was an infrastructure dependency
+   error, not evidence of passing or failing security analysis. Re-run
+   required before certifying the job.
+
+## Evidence links
+
+- Base mobile launch: [web startup test](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze/actions/runs/45160901451).
+- Mainline UX changes: [UI patch](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze/commit/1f0610c303613f62d96e86aa2403f1b20e2aaab3).
+- Flutter accessibility correction: [follow-up patch](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze/commit/fc4481fa2855bfd5037e5ae7f7084f8763e5ade3).
+- Audio-first changes awaiting integration: [PR #4](https://github.com/MetroKim/Breezze/pull/4).
+
+## Release blockers — do not silently mark complete
+
+- **P0:** Green Flutter analyzer, unit/widget, backend, edge worker and platform
+  build workflows on the same final commit; emulator playback and interruption
+  journeys; recoverable auth/search/playback flows.
+- **P0:** Legally approved full-track playback and offline download rights for
+  each provider, territory and recording; rights enforcement in the backend.
+  A free Deezer API does not grant whole-catalog streaming licenses.
+- **P1:** Account-isolation E2E tests for library/favorites/playlists/downloads,
+  logout/revocation, offline lease expiry, network recovery and background audio.
+- **P1:** Verify loading, empty, offline, error and success states for every
+  screen; screen-reader labels, dynamic type, 48dp touch targets, and
+  reduced-motion behavior on supported platforms.
+- **P1:** Resolve draft audio-first PR #4 and CI issues before declaring its
+  gapless, video handoff, dynamic theme or download changes shipped.
+- **P2:** Live production API/edge health, secrets, backups, operational alerts,
+  physical-device lock-screen and Bluetooth validation, capacity testing,
+  app-store signing/distribution and incident rollback.
+
+## Interface direction
+
+Breezze is a **restrained, premium, functional music application**—not a
+futuristic mockup. Retain the existing black/white Breezze identity, original
+SVG interface icons, semantic light/dark tokens, real album artwork,
+readable typography, consistent 8dp spacing, native input feedback and
+focused primary controls. Artwork-derived color may enrich Now Playing
+subtly, but must not reduce foreground contrast or replace opaque fallbacks.
+No decorative emoji, fake tracks, fabricated charts or broken CTA actions.
+
+**Completion policy:** A screen or endpoint is not finished merely because its
+widget, route, API stub or architecture file exists. Production-ready claims
+require verified action, authorized backend data, explicit failure/empty
+states, and automated/device evidence at the final commit.
+
+## Current UX and rendered-browser acceptance extension
+
+- Empty Now Playing state includes an accessible Close and Back to music
+  action, instead of leaving a listener in an undismissable full-screen shell.
+- The full-screen progress slider stages drag values locally and commits one
+  seek when released; unavailable duration disables seeking.
+- The navigation selection transition honors the OS reduce-motion preference,
+  and onboarding back navigation clamps to the first step.
+- The Flutter web preview workflow launches actual headless Chromium at a
+  390px mobile viewport, checks for a rendered Flutter scene, records uncaught
+  JavaScript exceptions, and publishes a screenshot artifact. This extends
+  but does not replace manual screen-reader/physical-device verification.
+
+## Additional runtime audit
+
+- Home and Library now finish initial loads even when local persistence fails,
+  displaying in-app retry actions instead of indefinite skeleton screens.
+- Concurrent Library refreshes reject stale account/generation results, and
+  switching users clears in-memory library content before asynchronous reload.
+- Malformed/empty stored section preferences fall back to known valid filters.
+- The API Worker handles preflight requests and adds CORS headers only for
+  explicitly configured trusted browser origins. The deployed origin/Worker
+  still require matching environment configuration and authorized deployment.
+- Browser UI verification checks the Flutter painter inside shadow DOM, not
+  only top-level canvas elements. The edge CORS failure in CI is tracked
+  separately and must be tested against the deployed allowed origin.
+
+- Added Node-based edge CORS regression coverage (exact origin, denied origin,
+  unsafe method, error responses), executed in the API Worker CI job.
+- The guarded API deployment workflow now passes the configured browser
+  allowlist to Cloudflare and verifies the preflight on the deployed endpoint.

@@ -45,3 +45,28 @@ Set `API_ORIGIN` as a Wrangler variable through the Cloudflare dashboard or depl
 The NestJS API requires the same `EDGE_SHARED_SECRET` in production, so direct requests to the origin are rejected. The Worker overwrites any client-supplied `x-breezze-edge-secret` header before forwarding and removes the header from responses.
 
 The deployment workflow deploys the Worker only. The NestJS origin must already be deployed and reachable over HTTPS, and DNS/custom-domain routing plus WAF/rate-limit rules still require Cloudflare account configuration. This does not prove one-million-user capacity; staged load tests are required.
+
+## Browser/Codespaces CORS policy
+
+Configure `WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` in the **Cloudflare Worker** as a
+comma-separated list of **exact** trusted frontend origins, for example a
+specific deployed web host and the specific Codespaces forwarded URL. Set the
+same origins in NestJS `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`, since the API has an independent
+origin allowlist. A development browser running at `http://127.0.0.1:8080`
+requires that exact origin in each allowlist; `http://localhost:8080` is a
+different origin. Never use a wildcard or reflect arbitrary origins with
+credentials enabled. Production defaults to no browser origins until explicitly
+configured. Both layers must be deployed for their configuration to take effect.
+
+The Worker now answers allowed preflight requests with narrowly limited
+methods and headers before the origin-secret check, and decorates successful
+**and error** API responses with CORS headers for allowed origins, so upstream
+outages do not masquerade as missing browser CORS. The backend remains the
+authority for authentication and resource authorization.
+
+The deploy workflow now accepts the repository Actions variable
+`BREEZZE_WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`, maps it to the Worker variable
+`WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`, and verifies a successful browser preflight after
+deployment. The protected NestJS origin must independently set
+`CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` to the corresponding trusted origins. Configure exact
+hostnames only; a Codespace forwarding URL may change between instances.

 const PUBLIC_CATALOG_PATH =
@@ -16,6 +18,60 @@ function jsonError(status: number, code: string): Response {
   );
 }
 
+
+// Browser origin allowlisting is distinct from server-to-server edge auth.
+// Never use '*' with credentialed requests or trust a client-supplied origin.
+function browserOrigin(request: Request, env: Env): string | null {
+  const origin = request.headers.get('Origin');
+  if (!origin) return null;
+  const allowed = (env.WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS ?? '')
+    .split(',').map((entry) => entry.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+  return allowed.includes(origin) ? origin : null;
+}
+
+function withBrowserCors(response: Response, request: Request, env: Env): Response {
+  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
+  const vary = headers.get('Vary');
+  if (request.headers.has('Origin')) {
+    const varyParts = (vary ?? '').split(',').map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+    if (!varyParts.some((item) => item.toLowerCase() === 'origin')) {
+      headers.set('Vary', [...varyParts, 'Origin'].join(', '));
+    }
+  }
+  const origin = browserOrigin(request, env);
+  if (origin) {
+    headers.set('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', origin);
+    headers.set('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', 'true');
+    headers.set('Access-Control-Expose-Headers', 'x-request-id, x-breezze-edge-cache, etag, content-range');
+  } else {
+    headers.delete('Access-Control-Allow-Origin');
+    headers.delete('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials');
+    headers.delete('Access-Control-Expose-Headers');
+  }
+  return new Response(response.body, {
+    status: response.status,
+    statusText: response.statusText,
+    headers,
+  });
+}
+
+function corsPreflight(request: Request, env: Env): Response | null {
+  if (request.method !== 'OPTIONS' || !request.headers.has('Access-Control-Request-Method')) {
+    return null;
+  }
+  const origin = browserOrigin(request, env);
+  const method = request.headers.get('Access-Control-Request-Method')?.toUpperCase();
+  if (!origin || !method || !['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'].includes(method)) {
+    return jsonError(403, 'CORS_ORIGIN_OR_METHOD_DENIED');
+  }
+  const response = withBrowserCors(new Response(null, { status: 204 }), request, env);
+  response.headers.set('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE');
+  response.headers.set('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'authorization, content-type');
+  response.headers.set('Access-Control-Max-Age', '600');
+  response.headers.set('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store');
+  return response;
+}
+
 function isPublicCatalogRequest(request: Request, url: URL): boolean {
   return request.method === 'GET'
     && PUBLIC_CATALOG_PATH.test(url.pathname)
@@ -157,7 +213,9 @@ async function handle(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promis
 }
 
 export default {
-  fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
-    return handle(request, env, ctx);
+  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
+    const preflight = corsPreflight(request, env);
+    if (preflight) return preflight;
+    return withBrowserCors(await handle(request, env, ctx), request, env);

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import test from 'node:test';
+import worker from '../src/index.ts';
+
+const knownOrigin = 'https://breezze.example';
+const env = {
+  EDGE_SHARED_SECRET: 'test-only-secret-not-deployed-0123456789',
+  WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: knownOrigin + ',http://127.0.0.1:8080',
+};
+const ctx = { waitUntil() {} };
+
+function preflight(origin, method = 'GET') {
+  return new Request('https://edge.example/api/v1/auth/social/providers', {
+    method: 'OPTIONS',
+    headers: {
+      Origin: origin,
+      'Access-Control-Request-Method': method,
+      'Access-Control-Request-Headers': 'content-type',
+    },
+  });
+}
+
+test('trusted web origin receives credentialed 204 preflight', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(preflight(knownOrigin, 'POST'), env, ctx);
+  assert.equal(response.status, 204);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin'), knownOrigin);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('access-control-allow-credentials'), 'true');
+  assert.match(response.headers.get('access-control-allow-headers'), /content-type/);
+  assert.match(response.headers.get('vary'), /Origin/i);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('cache-control'), 'private, no-store');
+});
+
+test('unlisted origin is never reflected', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(preflight('https://attacker.example'), env, ctx);
+  assert.equal(response.status, 403);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin'), null);
+});
+
+test('unsafe method is denied even for a listed origin', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(preflight(knownOrigin, 'TRACE'), env, ctx);
+  assert.equal(response.status, 403);
+});
+
+test('allowed origin receives CORS headers even on missing backend configuration', async () => {
+  const request = new Request('https://edge.example/api/v1/auth/social/providers', {
+    headers: { Origin: knownOrigin },
+  });
+  const response = await worker.fetch(request, env, ctx);
+  assert.equal(response.status, 503);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin'), knownOrigin);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('cache-control'), 'no-store');
+});
+
+test('unspecified browser origin does not accidentally enable CORS', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(
+    new Request('https://edge.example/api/v1/auth/social/providers'),
+    env,
+    ctx,
+  );
+  assert.equal(response.status, 503);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin'), null);
+});
+
+test('development loopback origin requires exact spelling', async () => {
+  const good = await worker.fetch(preflight('http://127.0.0.1:8080'), env, ctx);
+  const different = await worker.fetch(preflight('http://localhost:8080'), env, ctx);
+  assert.equal(good.status, 204);
+  assert.equal(different.status, 403);
+});

+// Only configured web applications may read signed stream responses in a browser.
+// Native clients send no Origin; they remain unaffected by this CORS policy.
+function browserOrigin(request: Request, env: Env): string | null {
+  const origin = request.headers.get('Origin');
+  if (!origin) return null;
+  const allowed = (env.WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS ?? '')
+    .split(',').map((value) => value.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+  return allowed.includes(origin) ? origin : null;
+}
+
+function withBrowserCors(response: Response, request: Request, env: Env): Response {
+  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
+  if (request.headers.has('Origin')) {
+    const values = (headers.get('Vary') ?? '').split(',').map(v => v.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+    if (!values.some(v => v.toLowerCase() === 'origin')) {
+      headers.set('Vary', [...values, 'Origin'].join(', '));
+    }
+    const origin = browserOrigin(request, env);
+    if (origin) {
+      headers.set('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', origin);
+      headers.set('Access-Control-Expose-Headers', 'Content-Length, Content-Range, ETag, Accept-Ranges');
+    } else {
+      headers.delete('Access-Control-Allow-Origin');
+    }
+  }
+  return new Response(response.body, { status: response.status, statusText: response.statusText, headers });
+}

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import test from 'node:test';
+import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
+import worker from '../src/index.ts';
+
+const key = 'this-test-signing-key-is-long-and-private';
+const trackId = 'test-track';
+const variant = 'AAC_256';
+const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode('0123456789');
+const calls = [];
+
+const head = {
+  size: bytes.length,
+  httpEtag: '"asset-v1"',
+  httpMetadata: { contentType: 'audio/mp4' },
+};
+function body(start, end) {
+  const sliced = bytes.slice(start, end);
+  return {
+    ...head,
+    body: new ReadableStream({
+      start(controller) {
+        controller.enqueue(sliced);
+        controller.close();
+      },
+    }),
+  };
+}
+const bucket = {
+  async head(objectKey) {
+    calls.push({ method: 'head', objectKey });
+    return objectKey === 'audio/test-track/AAC_256' ? head : null;
+  },
+  async get(objectKey, options) {
+    calls.push({ method: 'get', objectKey, options });
+    if (objectKey !== 'audio/test-track/AAC_256') return null;
+    const start = options?.range?.offset ?? 0;
+    const end = start + (options?.range?.length ?? bytes.length - start);
+    return body(start, end);
+  },
+};
+const env = { MEDIA_BUCKET: bucket, MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY: key };
+
+function url(expires = Date.now() + 45_000, sig) {
+  const signingPayload = `${trackId}:${variant}:${expires}`;
+  const signature = sig ?? createHmac('sha256', key).update(signingPayload).digest('base64url');
+  return `https://breezze-media.example/audio/${trackId}/${variant}?e=${expires}&s=${signature}`;
+}
+
+test('private media worker fails closed without R2 or signer', async () => {
+  const req = new Request(url());
+  assert.equal((await worker.fetch(req, { MEDIA_BUCKET: bucket })).status, 503);
+  assert.equal((await worker.fetch(req, { MEDIA_SIGNING_KEY: key })).status, 503);
+});
+
+test('bad and expired signatures never read storage', async () => {
+  calls.length = 0;
+  assert.equal((await worker.fetch(new Request(url(Date.now()+45000,'x'.repeat(43))),env)).status,403);
+  assert.equal((await worker.fetch(new Request(url(Date.now()-45000)),env)).status,403);
+  assert.equal(calls.length, 0);
+});
+
+test('valid, authorized URL streams private audio with no-store', async () => {
+  calls.length = 0;
+  const response = await worker.fetch(new Request(url()),env);
+  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
+  assert.equal(await response.text(), '0123456789');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Cache-Control'), 'private, no-store');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Content-Length'), '10');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('ETag'), '"asset-v1"');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'), null);
+  assert.deepEqual(calls.map(x=>x.objectKey), ['audio/test-track/AAC_256']);
+});
+
+test('GET Range returns exact bytes and proper 206 headers', async () => {
+  calls.length = 0;
+  const response = await worker.fetch(new Request(url(), {
+    headers: { Range: 'bytes=2-5' },
+  }),env);
+  assert.equal(response.status, 206);
+  assert.equal(await response.text(), '2345');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Content-Range'), 'bytes 2-5/10');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Content-Length'), '4');
+  assert.equal(calls.at(-1)?.options?.range?.offset, 2);
+  assert.equal(calls.at(-1)?.options?.range?.length, 4);
+});
+
+test('suffix byte range returns the final requested length', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(new Request(url(), {
+    headers: { Range: 'bytes=-3' },
+  }),env);
+  assert.equal(response.status,206);
+  assert.equal(await response.text(),'789');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Content-Range'),'bytes 7-9/10');
+});
+
+test('out of bounds multi-range fails closed', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(new Request(url(), {
+    headers: { Range: 'bytes=2-4,7-9' },
+  }),env);
+  assert.equal(response.status, 416);
+});
+
+test('HEAD serves metadata without a body', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(new Request(url(), {method:'HEAD'}),env);
+  assert.equal(response.status,200);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Content-Length'),'10');
+  assert.equal(await response.text(),'');
+});
+
+test('If-Range mismatch returns full 200 to prevent mixed audio assets', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(new Request(url(), {
+    headers: {Range:'bytes=2-4', 'If-Range':'"old-version"'},
+  }),env);
+  assert.equal(response.status,200);
+  assert.equal(await response.text(),'0123456789');
+});
+
+test('invalid path and methods cannot read files', async () => {
+  calls.length=0;
+  const invalid = new Request('https://breezze-media.example/audio/../AAC_256');
+  assert.equal((await worker.fetch(invalid,env)).status,404);
+  assert.equal((await worker.fetch(new Request(url(), {method:'POST'}),env)).status,405);
+  assert.equal(calls.length,0);
+});
+
+test('browser preflight permits only explicitly listed origins and range headers', async () => {
+  const browserEnv = { ...env, WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: 'https://breezze.example' };
+  const preflight = new Request(url(), {
+    method: 'OPTIONS',
+    headers: {
+      Origin: 'https://breezze.example',
+      'Access-Control-Request-Method': 'GET',
+      'Access-Control-Request-Headers': 'range',
+    },
+  });
+  const response = await worker.fetch(preflight, browserEnv);
+  assert.equal(response.status,204);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'),'https://breezze.example');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Vary'),'Origin');
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Headers'),'Range, If-Range');
+  assert.equal((await worker.fetch(new Request(url(), {
+    method:'OPTIONS',
+    headers:{
+      Origin:'https://attacker.example',
+      'Access-Control-Request-Method':'GET',
+    },
+  }),browserEnv)).status,403);
+});
+
+test('untrusted browser origin cannot read even a signed media URL', async () => {
+  calls.length = 0;
+  const response = await worker.fetch(new Request(url(),{
+    headers: { Origin:'https://attacker.example' },
+  }),{...env,WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS:'https://breezze.example'});
+  assert.equal(response.status,403);
+  assert.equal(calls.length,0);
+});
+
+test('authorized browser gets exposed seek metadata but no wildcard CORS', async () => {
+  const response = await worker.fetch(new Request(url(), {
+    headers:{ Origin:'https://breezze.example',Range:'bytes=0-1'},
+  }),{...env,WEB_ALLOWED_ORIGINS:'https://breezze.example'});
+  assert.equal(response.status,206);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Origin'),'https://breezze.example');
+  assert.match(response.headers.get('Access-Control-Expose-Headers') ?? '', /Content-Range/);
+  assert.equal(response.headers.get('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials'),null);
+});

+-- Apply only in a NEW Breezze Supabase project, before Prisma migrations.
+-- Keep Breezze private operational tables out of the Supabase Data API.
+CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS breezze;
+REVOKE ALL ON SCHEMA breezze FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
+-- Then configure Prisma DATABASE_URL with ?schema=breezze and
+-- run all Prisma migrations on the dedicated Supabase PostgreSQL database.
+-- Do not expose the 'breezze' schema in the Supabase Data API settings.

+  // R2 returns strong quoted ETags for each stored media version.

+    return match == null ? null : 'etag:${match.group(1)}';

+/// Optional native playlist capability. This is deliberately separate from
+/// AudioEngineAdapter: licensed remote streams still require per-track
+/// playback sessions, and cannot be speculatively stitched into a playlist.
+abstract interface class GaplessLocalQueueEngine implements AudioEngineAdapter {
+  /// True only when the platform provides a verified native playlist.

+

+    required int initialIndex,
+  });

+  Stream<int?> get currentIndexStream;
+
+  /// Seeks within an already prepared native playlist.
+  Future<void> skipToIndex(int index);

+  /// Syncs the app queue to a native decoder's automatic playlist index.
+  /// This does not perform a second audio seek or prepare.
+  bool selectEntry(String entryId) {
+    final index = _entries.indexWhere((entry) => entry.entryId == entryId);
+    if (index < 0) return false;
+    if (index == _currentIndex) return true;
+    if (current != null) _history.add(current!);
+    _currentIndex = index;
+    onQueueChanged?.call();
+    return true;
+  }

+/// Applies an icon-derived brand seed without sacrificing contrast or
+/// the intentionally monochrome Breezze identity.
+/// A black/white icon stays monochrome; colorful variants can adapt.
+BzThemeColors bzColorsFromBrandSeed({
+  required Brightness brightness,
+  Color? seedColor,
+}) {
+  final base = brightness == Brightness.dark
+      ? BzThemeColors.dark : BzThemeColors.light;
+  if (seedColor == null) return base;
+  final hsv = HSVColor.fromColor(seedColor);
+  if (hsv.saturation < 0.16 || hsv.value < 0.10) return base;
+
+  final generated = ColorScheme.fromSeed(
+    seedColor: seedColor,
+    brightness: brightness,
+  );
+  final accent = generated.primary;
+  final l1 = accent.computeLuminance();
+  final l2 = base.background.computeLuminance();
+  final contrast = (l1 > l2 ? l1 + 0.05 : l2 + 0.05) /
+      (l1 > l2 ? l2 + 0.05 : l1 + 0.05);
+  // Interactive text/icons need WCAG AA-level contrast.
+  if (contrast < 4.5) return base;
+  return base.copyWith(accent: accent);
+}
+
 /// The light theme, §21.2.
-ThemeData bzLightTheme() {
+ThemeData bzLightTheme({Color? brandSeed}) {
   final base = ThemeData.light(useMaterial3: true);
-  final c = BzThemeColors.light;
+  final c = bzColorsFromBrandSeed(
+    brightness: Brightness.light, seedColor: brandSeed,
+  );
   return base.copyWith(
     extensions: [c],
     scaffoldBackgroundColor: c.background,

FILE packages/design_system/test/bz_adaptive_theme_test.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,62 @@
+import 'package:breezze_design_system/breezze_design_system.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+import 'package:material_ui/material_ui.dart';
+
+void main() {
+  test('black and white icon colors preserve Breezze monochrome', () {
+    final dark = bzColorsFromBrandSeed(
+      brightness: Brightness.dark,
+      seedColor: const Color(0xFF000000),
+    );
+    final light = bzColorsFromBrandSeed(
+      brightness: Brightness.light,
+      seedColor: const Color(0xFFFFFFFF),
+    );
+    expect(dark.accent, BzThemeColors.dark.accent);
+    expect(light.accent, BzThemeColors.light.accent);
+  });
+
+  test('saturated brand seeds can adapt light and dark themes', () {
+    const seed = Color(0xFF2469E8);
+    final light = bzColorsFromBrandSeed(
+      brightness: Brightness.light, seedColor: seed,
+    );
+    final dark = bzColorsFromBrandSeed(
+      brightness: Brightness.dark, seedColor: seed,
+    );
+    expect(light.background, BzThemeColors.light.background);
+    expect(dark.background, BzThemeColors.dark.background);
+    expect(light.accent, isNot(BzThemeColors.light.accent));
+    expect(dark.accent, isNot(BzThemeColors.dark.accent));
+    expect(bzLightTheme(brandSeed: seed).bz.accent, light.accent);
+    expect(bzDarkTheme(brandSeed: seed).bz.accent, dark.accent);
+  });
+
+  test('contrast of adapted accents meets 4.5:1 on the main surface', () {
+    for (final brightness in [Brightness.light, Brightness.dark]) {
+      final c = bzColorsFromBrandSeed(
+        brightness: brightness, seedColor: const Color(0xFF2469E8),
+      );
+      final accent = c.accent.computeLuminance();
+      final background = c.background.computeLuminance();
+      final ratio = (accent > background
+          ? (accent + 0.05) / (background + 0.05)
+          : (background + 0.05) / (accent + 0.05));
+      expect(ratio, greaterThanOrEqualTo(4.5));
+    }
+  });
+
+  test('a missing or invalid seed retains the safe baseline', () {
+    expect(
+      bzColorsFromBrandSeed(brightness: Brightness.light).accent,
+      BzThemeColors.light.accent,
+    );
+    expect(
+      bzColorsFromBrandSeed(
+        brightness: Brightness.dark,
+        seedColor: const Color(0xFF111111),
+      ).accent,
+      BzThemeColors.dark.accent,
+    );
+  });
+}

FILE supabase/README.md
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+# Breezze — Supabase Auth only
+
+## Authentication decision
+
+Supabase Auth is Breezze's sole external identity authority. Native Google and Apple sign-in are the primary buttons on the existing Flutter Breezze AuthScreen. Email/password signup, verification and recovery are secondary. No Auth0, Melody Auth, or independently managed social OAuth login remains in the active app.
+
+Dedicated project: Breezze in Allen's Org, London (eu-west-2), reference djwvyjtebmsavhcrmhxf. Dashboard: https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/djwvyjtebmsavhcrmhxf . Initial project cost quote: $0/month. Free tier currently lists 50,000 monthly active users, with other usage caps. The project is ACTIVE_HEALTHY. Its private breezze database schema exists, with anon/authenticated roles denied USAGE. Railway Postgres is still the existing app data source until a separately verified migration.
+
+Supabase Auth origin: https://djwvyjtebmsavhcrmhxf.supabase.co
+Flutter public publishable key: sb_publishable_ZloCmmQPo86OqHbH3Yqgsw_xZGPqSi3
+Server Railway values required: SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY, matching the project above. Never put a service-role or secret key in Flutter, a repository, or a URL.
+
+## Native Google and Apple
+
+The Flutter implementation uses native Google and Apple SDKs to obtain provider ID tokens, then exchanges them with Supabase Auth using the ID-token grant. NestJS verifies the new Supabase user access token through Supabase Auth /auth/v1/user and issues Breezze's existing short-lived revocable session. App account IDs and ownership remain in Breezze.
+
+Google Android/iOS: configure OAuth Android SHA fingerprints, iOS bundle identifier and required URL scheme, and a Google Cloud Web OAuth Client ID within Supabase Authentication > Providers > Google. Supply BREEZZE_GOOGLE_WEB_CLIENT_ID to Flutter, and BREEZZE_GOOGLE_IOS_CLIENT_ID for iOS if needed. Review Supabase's native Google nonce configuration before live sign-in.
+
+Apple iOS/macOS: configure the Apple Developer account, bundle ID, Sign in with Apple capability and provisioning, plus Supabase Authentication > Providers > Apple. Only enable Flutter BREEZZE_APPLE_SIGN_IN_ENABLED=true after live configuration and native testing. Apple Developer membership may cost money even while Supabase Auth is on Free. Apple uses a random nonce; send its SHA-256 hash to Apple and the raw nonce to Supabase.
+
+Other operating systems require a separately reviewed OAuth redirect/deep-link implementation for social sign-in; the unsupported native buttons are disabled instead of opening an untested browser flow. Email/password remains usable in Breezze's existing Flutter layout. Social providers are not live simply because buttons compile.
+
+## Secure legacy library migration
+
+The historical Railway account owns an existing User ID, favorites, saved albums, and playlists. SupabaseAuthIdentity maps verified Supabase subjects to Breezze User IDs; matching email alone never proves ownership. POST /api/v1/auth/supabase/exchange is the only new sign-in exchange route. Temporary POST /api/v1/auth/supabase/claim requires both Supabase verified identity and the legacy Breezze Argon2id password and is gated by SUPABASE_LEGACY_CLAIM_ENABLED=true. Once migrated, the listener keeps their old Breezze account ID and library.
+
+Internal Breezze access and refresh JWTs remain in the app service for backwards-compatible revocation and authorization. They are not a second login provider. Historical account rows, inactive social tables and previous schema migrations must be retained for a non-destructive migration and rollback; they are not active app login routes.
+
+Password recovery uses Supabase Auth. Until full third-party identity deletion is implemented, the account deletion UI routes to Help & Support rather than incorrectly asking a Supabase user for a legacy Breezze password.
+
+## Release gates
+
+1. Enable Google and Apple provider configuration in the real Supabase project and verify native sign-in on iOS and Android emulators.
+2. Configure matching public Supabase URL/key in Railway, apply the Prisma SupabaseAuthIdentity migration to the currently authoritative application database, verify NestJS token exchange.
+3. Verify both fresh accounts and existing-account claims; ensure user A cannot see user B's library. Check email confirmation/reset, revoke sessions, and logout.
+4. Pass exact-head CI typechecks, tests, Android/iOS and desktop builds. Keep the integration PR in draft until verified.
+5. Back up and reconcile production data before any switch to Supabase PostgreSQL.
+
+Cloudflare API edge and private R2 music delivery remain unrelated to identity. No media playback licensing or downloader permissions change as part of this authentication migration.
+
+Status: integration code staged; Google/Apple provider client credentials are NOT configured or live-verified and no production auth cutover has occurred.

+-- Optional private listener-avatar storage in the dedicated Breezze Supabase project.
+-- Full-track audio lives exclusively in Cloudflare R2, NOT Supabase Storage.
+-- Do not apply this to the unrelated 'metro bot' project.
+INSERT INTO storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
+VALUES (
+  'breezze-avatars', 'breezze-avatars', false, 5242880,
+  ARRAY['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp']
+)
+ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE
+SET public = false,
+    file_size_limit = EXCLUDED.file_size_limit,
+    allowed_mime_types = EXCLUDED.allowed_mime_types;
+
+-- There is no direct client upload policy yet because Breezze's existing
+-- session IDs have not been migrated to Supabase Auth. Once that migration
+-- succeeds, add explicit auth.uid()-ownership policies for user folders.
+-- Until then, the backend service role alone may access profile avatars.
+-- Restrictive policy protects this bucket even if the project later has
+-- a permissive policy for unrelated Storage resources.
+CREATE POLICY breezze_avatar_no_direct_client_access
+ON storage.objects AS RESTRICTIVE
+FOR ALL TO anon, authenticated
+USING (bucket_id <> 'breezze-avatars')
+WITH CHECK (bucket_id <> 'breezze-avatars');
